  block-diagonal mask, shared Π KV, restarted positions; a margin certificate (max |Δlogit| < min margin) per row.

scan_job.py:96:def time_chunk(model, table):
scan_job.py:97:    """One packed forward of a full chunk, every slot the same row. Returns seconds and whether every slot hit."""
scan_job.py:98:    past = fwd.prefill(model, table["pi"])
scan_job.py:99:    q = table["queries"][0]
scan_job.py:100:    target = table["rows"][0][table["block_len"]]
scan_job.py:101:    blocks = [q] * fwd.chunk_for(table["block_len"])
scan_job.py:102:    summary, seconds = fwd.packed_lookup(model, past, len(table["pi"]), blocks)
scan_job.py:103:    n_match = sum(arg == target and margin > 0 for arg, margin in summary)
scan_job.py:104:    min_margin = min(margin for _, margin in summary) if summary else None
scan_job.py:105:    del past
scan_job.py:106:    return {"seconds": seconds, "n": len(summary), "n_match": n_match, "min_margin": min_margin,
scan_job.py:107:            "block_len": table["block_len"], "P": len(table["pi"])}
scan_job.py:108:
scan_job.py:109:
scan_job.py:110:def extrapolate(chunk_seconds):
scan_job.py:111:    """Hillis–Steele model forwards at 100000 digits, using one measured full-chunk forward as the unit."""
scan_job.py:112:    look = alg.lookup_counts(100000)
scan_job.py:113:    unit = max(chunk_seconds)
scan_job.py:114:    forwards = (
scan_job.py:115:        math.ceil(look["d"] / fwd.chunk_for(2))
scan_job.py:116:        + math.ceil(look["hillis_m"] / fwd.chunk_for(2))
scan_job.py:117:        + math.ceil(look["s"] / fwd.chunk_for(4))
scan_job.py:118:    )
scan_job.py:119:    return {"unit_seconds": unit, "forwards": forwards, "extrapolated_hs_seconds": unit * forwards,
scan_job.py:120:            "lookups": look}

HANDOFF_PAGE.md:1:# Packed lookups for a browser
HANDOFF_PAGE.md:2:
HANDOFF_PAGE.md:3:No site change ships with this. A browser that wants the same scan has to run the packed forward itself.
HANDOFF_PAGE.md:4:
HANDOFF_PAGE.md:5:The cached Qwen/Qwen3-4B config agrees with the job record on ('n_layers', 'n_kv_heads', 'head_dim', 'hidden', 'vocab'): True.
HANDOFF_PAGE.md:6:
HANDOFF_PAGE.md:7:Architecture source: the job record. Layers 36, query heads 32, KV heads 8, head dim 128, hidden 2560, vocab 151936, dtype bfloat16, sliding window False.
HANDOFF_PAGE.md:8:
HANDOFF_PAGE.md:9:KV bytes per token are layers × 2 (key and value) × KV heads × head dim × bytes per element: 36 × 2 × 8 × 128 × 2 = 147456.
HANDOFF_PAGE.md:10:
HANDOFF_PAGE.md:11:What the forward does:
HANDOFF_PAGE.md:12:
HANDOFF_PAGE.md:13:- Prefill Π once. Keep that KV. Every later forward clones it and discards the clone, so Π is not rewritten.
HANDOFF_PAGE.md:14:- A block is one lookup. D and M blocks are 2 tokens. S blocks are 4 tokens (carry-in, digit, digit, carry-out).
HANDOFF_PAGE.md:15:- position ids inside a block restart at |Π|, the same positions as the pinned single tick. The cache itself appends in order. The restarted positions are RoPE positions, not the write index.
HANDOFF_PAGE.md:16:- The mask is 4-dimensional, shape (1, 1, query tokens, |Π| + query tokens), boolean, True where attention is allowed. Each query may attend to every Π key and, causally, to the keys of its own block. It may not attend to any other block. One block reproduces the pinned tick.
HANDOFF_PAGE.md:17:- The argmax is read at the last token of each block. The logits of the other tokens can be dropped.
HANDOFF_PAGE.md:18:
HANDOFF_PAGE.md:19:Π lengths from the algebra record: D 400 tokens, M 36 tokens, S 1200 tokens. The deployed chunk is 4096 query tokens.
HANDOFF_PAGE.md:20:
HANDOFF_PAGE.md:21:Resident KV for one deployed forward, Π plus one chunk:
HANDOFF_PAGE.md:22:- D: 400 + 4096 = 4496 tokens, 662962176 bytes.
HANDOFF_PAGE.md:23:- M: 36 + 4096 = 4132 tokens, 609288192 bytes.
HANDOFF_PAGE.md:24:- S: 1200 + 4096 = 5296 tokens, 780926976 bytes.
HANDOFF_PAGE.md:25:
HANDOFF_PAGE.md:26:A single forward of the widest Hillis–Steele round at 4096 digits holds 4095 lookups × 2 tokens = 8190 query tokens, plus the M prefix of 36 tokens: 8226 tokens, 1212973056 bytes. The run does not do that. It cuts the round into chunks of the deployed width, and the resident set is the chunk line above. The browser can do the same.
scan_algebra.py:1:"""Carry-lookahead addition as a prefix scan over the {K, P, G} monoid.
scan_algebra.py:2:
scan_algebra.py:3:Pure Python. The model runner (scan_forward.py) executes the same schedules.
scan_algebra.py:4:K kills a carry, P propagates it, G generates one. Columns are least-significant first.
scan_algebra.py:5:
scan_algebra.py:6:D: digit pair (a, b) -> K if a+b <= 8, P if a+b == 9, G if a+b >= 10. 100 rows.
scan_algebra.py:7:M: (low, high) -> high if high is K or G, else low. 9 rows. P is the identity.
scan_algebra.py:8:S: the certified adder's (carry, a, b, carry_out, sum_digit). 200 rows.
scan_algebra.py:9:
scan_algebra.py:10:A sum of two n-digit operands is n columns. The final carry is a leading 1 when the
scan_algebra.py:11:full prefix is G. There is no extra (0, 0) column: the sequential clock's extra pair
scan_algebra.py:12:only flushes that same carry onto the tape.
scan_algebra.py:13:"""

scan_algebra.py:41:    """Monoid product. `low` is the less significant block, `high` the more significant one."""
scan_algebra.py:42:    if high == P:
scan_algebra.py:43:        return low
scan_algebra.py:44:    if high in (K, G):
scan_algebra.py:45:        return high
scan_algebra.py:46:    raise ValueError(high)

scan_algebra.py:76:def light_failures(op):
scan_algebra.py:77:    """Light's associativity test: the triples (x, y, z) with (x*y)*z != x*(y*z)."""
scan_algebra.py:78:    fails = []
scan_algebra.py:79:    for x in SYMBOLS:
scan_algebra.py:80:        for y in SYMBOLS:
scan_algebra.py:81:            for z in SYMBOLS:
scan_algebra.py:82:                left = op(op(x, y), z)
scan_algebra.py:83:                right = op(x, op(y, z))
scan_algebra.py:84:                if left != right:
scan_algebra.py:85:                    fails.append((x, y, z))
scan_algebra.py:86:    return fails

scan_algebra.py:89:def corruptions():
scan_algebra.py:90:    """The 18 single-row corruptions: each of 9 rows replaced by each of the 2 wrong symbols."""
scan_algebra.py:91:    base = true_table()
scan_algebra.py:92:    out = []
scan_algebra.py:93:    for low in SYMBOLS:
scan_algebra.py:94:        for high in SYMBOLS:
scan_algebra.py:95:            truth = base[(low, high)]
scan_algebra.py:96:            for wrong in SYMBOLS:
scan_algebra.py:97:                if wrong == truth:
scan_algebra.py:98:                    continue
scan_algebra.py:99:                table = dict(base)
scan_algebra.py:100:                table[(low, high)] = wrong
scan_algebra.py:101:                fails = light_failures(lambda x, y, table=table: table[(x, y)])
scan_algebra.py:102:                out.append({
scan_algebra.py:103:                    "low": low,
scan_algebra.py:104:                    "high": high,
scan_algebra.py:105:                    "true": truth,
scan_algebra.py:106:                    "wrong": wrong,
scan_algebra.py:107:                    "table": {f"{a}{b}": table[(a, b)] for a in SYMBOLS for b in SYMBOLS},
scan_algebra.py:108:                    "fails": ["".join(t) for t in fails],
scan_algebra.py:109:                    "n_fail": len(fails),
scan_algebra.py:110:                    "detectable": len(fails) > 0,
scan_algebra.py:111:                })
scan_algebra.py:112:    return out

scan_algebra.py:115:def signature_groups(corrs=None):
scan_algebra.py:116:    """Map a failing-triple signature to the corruptions that produce it."""
scan_algebra.py:117:    groups = {}
scan_algebra.py:118:    for c in corrs if corrs is not None else corruptions():
scan_algebra.py:119:        sig = tuple(c["fails"])
scan_algebra.py:120:        groups.setdefault(sig, []).append(c)
scan_algebra.py:121:    return groups

scan_algebra.py:127:def hillis_schedule(n):
scan_algebra.py:128:    """Inclusive Hillis–Steele. Each round is a list of ('lookup', dest, low_index, high_index).
scan_algebra.py:129:
scan_algebra.py:130:    Reads are from the array at the start of the round. Round count is ceil(log2 n) for n >= 1
scan_algebra.py:131:    (0 when n <= 1).
scan_algebra.py:132:    """
scan_algebra.py:133:    rounds = []
scan_algebra.py:134:    offset = 1
scan_algebra.py:135:    while offset < n:
scan_algebra.py:136:        rounds.append([("lookup", i, i - offset, i) for i in range(offset, n)])
scan_algebra.py:137:        offset *= 2
scan_algebra.py:138:    return rounds
scan_algebra.py:139:
scan_algebra.py:140:
scan_algebra.py:141:def blelloch_schedule(n):
scan_algebra.py:142:    """Inclusive Blelloch on the next power of two, padded with the identity P.
scan_algebra.py:143:
scan_algebra.py:144:    Returns (pad_length, rounds). Rounds may contain
scan_algebra.py:145:    ('lookup', dest, i, j), ('copy', dest, src), ('set_identity', index),
scan_algebra.py:146:    ('lookup_orig', dest, excl_index).
scan_algebra.py:147:    'lookup_orig' multiplies the exclusive value by the ORIGINAL padded element.
scan_algebra.py:148:    'set_identity' is not a model lookup.
scan_algebra.py:149:    """
scan_algebra.py:150:    pad = 1
scan_algebra.py:151:    while pad < n:
scan_algebra.py:152:        pad *= 2
scan_algebra.py:153:    rounds = []
scan_algebra.py:154:    logn = pad.bit_length() - 1
scan_algebra.py:155:    for d in range(logn):
scan_algebra.py:156:        step = 1 << (d + 1)
scan_algebra.py:157:        half = 1 << d
scan_algebra.py:158:        ops = []
scan_algebra.py:159:        for k in range(0, pad, step):
scan_algebra.py:160:            left = k + half - 1
scan_algebra.py:161:            right = k + step - 1
scan_algebra.py:162:            ops.append(("lookup", right, left, right))
scan_algebra.py:163:        rounds.append(ops)
scan_algebra.py:164:    rounds.append([("set_identity", pad - 1)])
scan_algebra.py:165:    for d in range(logn - 1, -1, -1):
scan_algebra.py:166:        step = 1 << (d + 1)
scan_algebra.py:167:        half = 1 << d
scan_algebra.py:168:        ops = []
scan_algebra.py:169:        for k in range(0, pad, step):
scan_algebra.py:170:            left = k + half - 1
scan_algebra.py:171:            right = k + step - 1
scan_algebra.py:172:            ops.append(("copy", left, right))
scan_algebra.py:173:            # Down-sweep: the right child must become (parent exclusive) ⊕ (left reduction).
scan_algebra.py:174:            # Parent exclusive sits at `right`, left reduction at `left`. The operator does not commute,
scan_algebra.py:175:            # so the low operand is `right` and the high operand is `left`. The copy above reads the snapshot.
scan_algebra.py:176:            ops.append(("lookup", right, right, left))
scan_algebra.py:177:        rounds.append(ops)
scan_algebra.py:178:    rounds.append([("lookup_orig", i, i) for i in range(pad)])
scan_algebra.py:179:    return pad, rounds
scan_algebra.py:180:
scan_algebra.py:181:
scan_algebra.py:182:def attention_allows(P, n_blocks, block_len, q, k):
scan_algebra.py:183:    """True when packed query `q` may attend to key `k`.
scan_algebra.py:184:
scan_algebra.py:185:    Keys 0..P-1 are Π. Keys P.. are the blocks, `block_len` each. Query q attends to every Π key and to the
scan_algebra.py:186:    keys of its own block up to itself. This is the pinned tick's causal pattern when n_blocks is 1.
scan_algebra.py:187:    """
scan_algebra.py:188:    Q = n_blocks * block_len
scan_algebra.py:189:    if not (0 <= q < Q) or k < 0 or k >= P + Q:
scan_algebra.py:190:        return False
scan_algebra.py:191:    if k < P:
scan_algebra.py:192:        return True
scan_algebra.py:193:    bq = q // block_len
scan_algebra.py:194:    bk = (k - P) // block_len
scan_algebra.py:195:    if bq != bk:
scan_algebra.py:196:        return False
scan_algebra.py:197:    return (k - P) % block_len <= q % block_len
scan_algebra.py:198:
scan_algebra.py:199:
scan_algebra.py:200:def allow_count(P, n_blocks, block_len):
scan_algebra.py:201:    Q = n_blocks * block_len
scan_algebra.py:202:    return sum(1 for q in range(Q) for k in range(P + Q) if attention_allows(P, n_blocks, block_len, q, k))

scan_algebra.py:205:def model_rounds(rounds):
scan_algebra.py:206:    """Rounds that contain at least one model lookup."""
scan_algebra.py:207:    return [r for r in rounds if any(op[0] in ("lookup", "lookup_orig") for op in r)]

scan_algebra.py:210:def plan_round(old, original, rnd):
scan_algebra.py:211:    """Reads for one round, taken from the snapshot `old` (and `original` for the inclusive step).
scan_algebra.py:212:
scan_algebra.py:213:    Each action is ('lookup', dest, low, high) or ('value', dest, value) or ('identity', dest).
scan_algebra.py:214:    The model runner and `eval_schedule` both consume this, so the index arithmetic lives in one place.
scan_algebra.py:215:    """
scan_algebra.py:216:    actions = []
scan_algebra.py:217:    for opnd in rnd:
scan_algebra.py:218:        kind = opnd[0]
scan_algebra.py:219:        if kind == "lookup":
scan_algebra.py:220:            _, dest, i, j = opnd
scan_algebra.py:221:            actions.append(("lookup", dest, old[i], old[j]))
scan_algebra.py:222:        elif kind == "lookup_orig":
scan_algebra.py:223:            _, dest, i = opnd
scan_algebra.py:224:            actions.append(("lookup", dest, old[i], original[i]))
scan_algebra.py:225:        elif kind == "copy":
scan_algebra.py:226:            _, dest, src = opnd
scan_algebra.py:227:            actions.append(("value", dest, old[src]))
scan_algebra.py:228:        elif kind == "set_identity":
scan_algebra.py:229:            actions.append(("identity", opnd[1]))
scan_algebra.py:230:        else:
scan_algebra.py:231:            raise ValueError(kind)
scan_algebra.py:232:    return actions
scan_algebra.py:233:
scan_algebra.py:234:
scan_algebra.py:235:def eval_schedule(values, rounds, op, identity=P):
scan_algebra.py:236:    """Run a schedule. `values` are symbols. Returns the array after the last round (padded length
scan_algebra.py:237:    for Blelloch) and the number of lookups."""
scan_algebra.py:238:    a = list(values)
scan_algebra.py:239:    original = list(values)
scan_algebra.py:240:    lookups = 0
scan_algebra.py:241:    for rnd in rounds:
scan_algebra.py:242:        new = list(a)
scan_algebra.py:243:        for action in plan_round(a, original, rnd):
scan_algebra.py:244:            kind = action[0]
scan_algebra.py:245:            if kind == "lookup":
scan_algebra.py:246:                _, dest, low, high = action
scan_algebra.py:247:                new[dest] = op(low, high)
scan_algebra.py:248:                lookups += 1
scan_algebra.py:249:            elif kind == "value":
scan_algebra.py:250:                _, dest, value = action
scan_algebra.py:251:                new[dest] = value
scan_algebra.py:252:            elif kind == "identity":
scan_algebra.py:253:                new[action[1]] = identity
scan_algebra.py:254:            else:
scan_algebra.py:255:                raise ValueError(kind)
scan_algebra.py:256:        a = new
scan_algebra.py:257:    return a, lookups

scan_algebra.py:260:def sequential_prefix(blocks, op=combine):
scan_algebra.py:261:    if not blocks:
scan_algebra.py:262:        return []
scan_algebra.py:263:    out = [blocks[0]]
scan_algebra.py:264:    for b in blocks[1:]:
scan_algebra.py:265:        out.append(op(out[-1], b))
scan_algebra.py:266:    return out

scan_algebra.py:271:    got, lookups = eval_schedule(blocks, rounds, op)
scan_algebra.py:272:    return got, len(model_rounds(rounds)), lookups

scan_algebra.py:311:def digits_from_prefixes(pairs, prefixes):
scan_algebra.py:312:    """Sum digits, least-significant first, and the final carry. Carry-in to column 0 is 0.
scan_algebra.py:313:    Carry-out of a prefix is 1 exactly when the prefix is G."""
scan_algebra.py:314:    cin = 0

scan_algebra.py:316:    cins = []

scan_algebra.py:318:        cins.append(cin)
scan_algebra.py:319:        digits.append((cin + a + b) % 10)
scan_algebra.py:320:        cin = 1 if prefixes[i] == G else 0
scan_algebra.py:321:    return digits, cins, cin

scan_algebra.py:328:    text = "".join(reversed(chars)).lstrip("0")
scan_algebra.py:329:    return text or "0"

scan_algebra.py:380:def replay_uses(blocks, kind, op=combine):
scan_algebra.py:381:    """Every model lookup `(dest, low, high)` under a schedule, and the final prefixes of length n.
scan_algebra.py:382:
scan_algebra.py:383:    `dest` is an index into the (possibly padded) array. Columns are `0 .. n-1`.
scan_algebra.py:384:    """
scan_algebra.py:385:    n = len(blocks)
scan_algebra.py:386:    if kind == "hs":
scan_algebra.py:387:        rounds = hillis_schedule(n)
scan_algebra.py:388:        values = list(blocks)
scan_algebra.py:389:    elif kind == "bl":
scan_algebra.py:390:        pad, rounds = blelloch_schedule(n)
scan_algebra.py:391:        values = list(blocks) + [P] * (pad - n)
scan_algebra.py:392:    else:
scan_algebra.py:393:        raise ValueError(kind)
scan_algebra.py:394:    a = list(values)
scan_algebra.py:395:    original = list(values)
scan_algebra.py:396:    uses = []
scan_algebra.py:397:    for rnd in rounds:
scan_algebra.py:398:        actions = plan_round(a, original, rnd)
scan_algebra.py:399:        new = list(a)
scan_algebra.py:400:        for act in actions:
scan_algebra.py:401:            if act[0] == "lookup":
scan_algebra.py:402:                _, dest, low, high = act
scan_algebra.py:403:                uses.append((dest, low, high))
scan_algebra.py:404:                new[dest] = op(low, high)
scan_algebra.py:405:            elif act[0] == "value":
scan_algebra.py:406:                new[act[1]] = act[2]
scan_algebra.py:407:            elif act[0] == "identity":
scan_algebra.py:408:                new[act[1]] = P
scan_algebra.py:409:            else:
scan_algebra.py:410:                raise ValueError(act[0])
scan_algebra.py:411:        a = new
scan_algebra.py:412:    return a[:n], uses

scan_algebra.py:415:def propagation(a_text, b_text, low, high, wrong):
scan_algebra.py:416:    """Where a corrupted row moves the sum, under the sequential fold and both schedules.
scan_algebra.py:417:
scan_algebra.py:418:    A sum digit changes exactly when its carry-in changes. A column is strictly downstream of a use when
scan_algebra.py:419:    some use of the corrupted row has `dest < column` (that lookup writes an earlier prefix).
scan_algebra.py:420:    """
scan_algebra.py:421:    op = lambda x, y, table=corrupted_table(low, high, wrong): table[(x, y)]
scan_algebra.py:422:    pairs = columns(a_text, b_text)
scan_algebra.py:423:    blocks = [classify(x, y) for x, y in pairs]
scan_algebra.py:424:    true_pref = sequential_prefix(blocks, combine)
scan_algebra.py:425:    out = {"n": len(blocks)}
scan_algebra.py:426:    for kind in ("seq", "hs", "bl"):
scan_algebra.py:427:        if kind == "seq":
scan_algebra.py:428:            pref = sequential_prefix(blocks, op)
scan_algebra.py:429:            uses = [(i, lo, hi) for i, lo, hi in sequential_uses(blocks, op)]
scan_algebra.py:430:        else:
scan_algebra.py:431:            pref, uses = replay_uses(blocks, kind, op)
scan_algebra.py:432:        used = sorted(dest for dest, lo, hi in uses if lo == low and hi == high and dest < len(blocks))
scan_algebra.py:433:        cin_diff = []
scan_algebra.py:434:        for i in range(len(blocks)):
scan_algebra.py:435:            true_cin = 0 if i == 0 else carry_bit(true_pref[i - 1])
scan_algebra.py:436:            bad_cin = 0 if i == 0 else carry_bit(pref[i - 1])
scan_algebra.py:437:            if true_cin != bad_cin:
scan_algebra.py:438:                cin_diff.append(i)
scan_algebra.py:439:        used_set = set(used)
scan_algebra.py:440:        cin_set = set(cin_diff)
scan_algebra.py:441:        out[kind] = {
scan_algebra.py:442:            "n_used": len(used),
scan_algebra.py:443:            "n_cin_diff": len(cin_diff),
scan_algebra.py:444:            "cin_diff_equals_used": cin_diff == used,
scan_algebra.py:445:            "every_wrong_column_after_a_use": all(any(u < i for u in used) for i in cin_diff),
scan_algebra.py:446:            "every_use_poisons_the_next_column": all((d + 1) in cin_set for d in used if d + 1 < len(blocks)),
scan_algebra.py:447:            "n_wrong_beyond_the_next_column": sum(1 for i in cin_diff if (i - 1) not in used_set),
scan_algebra.py:448:            "final_carry_differs": carry_bit(true_pref[-1]) != carry_bit(pref[-1]) if pref else False,
scan_algebra.py:449:        }
scan_algebra.py:450:    return out

scan_algebra.py:453:def hs_forward_rounds(n):
scan_algebra.py:454:    """D + Hillis–Steele + S."""
scan_algebra.py:455:    return (0 if n <= 1 else math.ceil(math.log2(n))) + 2
scan_algebra.py:456:
scan_algebra.py:457:
scan_algebra.py:458:def blelloch_forward_rounds(n):
scan_algebra.py:459:    """D + Blelloch model rounds + S. Blelloch model rounds = 2 * log2(pad) + 1, except n == 0."""
scan_algebra.py:460:    if n <= 0:
scan_algebra.py:461:        return 2
scan_algebra.py:462:    pad = 1
scan_algebra.py:463:    while pad < n:
scan_algebra.py:464:        pad *= 2
scan_algebra.py:465:    scan_rounds = (0 if pad == 1 else 2 * (pad.bit_length() - 1)) + 1
scan_algebra.py:466:    # n == 1: pad 1, log 0, up-sweep 0, down-sweep 0, inclusive 1. scan_rounds = 1. Good.
scan_algebra.py:467:    return scan_rounds + 2

scan_algebra.py:470:def lookup_counts(n):
scan_algebra.py:471:    """Model lookups for one addition, both schedules, D and S included once each (they are shared
scan_algebra.py:472:    conceptually but each schedule run pays for its own D and S if run separately)."""
scan_algebra.py:473:    blocks = [P] * n if n else []
scan_algebra.py:474:    # counts do not depend on the block values for these schedules
scan_algebra.py:475:    _, _, hs_look = hillis_prefix(blocks) if n else ([], 0, 0)
scan_algebra.py:476:    _, _, bl_look = blelloch_prefix(blocks) if n else ([], 0, 0)
scan_algebra.py:477:    return {"hillis_m": hs_look, "blelloch_m": bl_look, "d": n, "s": n}

scan_algebra.py:483:def sequential_uses(blocks, op):
scan_algebra.py:484:    """Lookups of the left-associated product. Entry i (i >= 1) is the lookup that writes prefix i."""
scan_algebra.py:485:    if not blocks:
scan_algebra.py:486:        return []
scan_algebra.py:487:    uses = []
scan_algebra.py:488:    acc = blocks[0]
scan_algebra.py:489:    for i in range(1, len(blocks)):
scan_algebra.py:490:        low, high = acc, blocks[i]
scan_algebra.py:491:        uses.append((i, low, high))
scan_algebra.py:492:        acc = op(low, high)
scan_algebra.py:493:    return uses

scan_algebra.py:496:def wrong_digit_positions(true_digits, true_final, bad_digits, bad_final):
scan_algebra.py:497:    """Positions in the least-significant-first digit list, plus 'final' if the leading carry differs.
scan_algebra.py:498:    Compared on the aligned numeric digit strings so a longer result is visible."""
scan_algebra.py:499:    t = number_from_digits(true_digits, true_final)
scan_algebra.py:500:    b = number_from_digits(bad_digits, bad_final)
scan_algebra.py:501:    width = max(len(t), len(b))
scan_algebra.py:502:    tt, bb = t.zfill(width), b.zfill(width)
scan_algebra.py:503:    # index 0 is the most significant in this alignment; also return LSB-index form
scan_algebra.py:504:    msb_diff = [i for i in range(width) if tt[i] != bb[i]]
scan_algebra.py:505:    return {"true": t, "bad": b, "n_diff_digits": len(msb_diff), "width": width}

scan_algebra.py:510:    return [i for i, lo, hi in uses if lo == low and hi == high]

scan_algebra.py:513:def affected_columns(blocks, table, low, high):

scan_algebra.py:522:    # a column's sum digit depends on the carry in, i.e. the previous prefix (or nothing at 0)

scan_algebra.py:537:    whose failing-triple signature is unique among the 18, and which changes instance 0's sum.

scan_algebra.py:548:        if len(groups[sig]) != 1:

scan_algebra.py:577:    """The carry a prefix emits when the carry into the whole span is 0."""

scan_algebra.py:585:def necessity_inputs():
scan_algebra.py:586:    """One 2-column input per monoid row.
scan_algebra.py:587:
scan_algebra.py:588:    A wrong output changes some sum exactly when it leaves the carry class of the true output
scan_algebra.py:589:    (G versus K or P). Swapping K with P does not change any sum: both emit carry 0, and every
scan_algebra.py:590:    right-multiplication preserves that agreement. See the theory note.
scan_algebra.py:591:    """
scan_algebra.py:592:    force = {K: (1, 1), P: (9, 0), G: (5, 5)}
scan_algebra.py:593:    high_pair = {K: (1, 0), P: (4, 5), G: (9, 1)}
scan_algebra.py:594:    out = []
scan_algebra.py:595:    base = true_table()
scan_algebra.py:596:    for low in SYMBOLS:
scan_algebra.py:597:        for high in SYMBOLS:
scan_algebra.py:598:            a0, b0 = force[low]
scan_algebra.py:599:            a1, b1 = high_pair[high]
scan_algebra.py:600:            a = str(a0 + 10 * a1)
scan_algebra.py:601:            b = str(b0 + 10 * b1)
scan_algebra.py:602:            blocks = [classify(a0, b0), classify(a1, b1)]
scan_algebra.py:603:            assert blocks == [low, high], blocks
scan_algebra.py:604:            uses = sequential_uses(blocks, combine)
scan_algebra.py:605:            assert uses == [(1, low, high)]
scan_algebra.py:606:            true_sum = bigint_sum(a, b)
scan_algebra.py:607:            wrongs = []
scan_algebra.py:608:            for wrong in SYMBOLS:
scan_algebra.py:609:                if wrong == base[(low, high)]:
scan_algebra.py:610:                    continue
scan_algebra.py:611:                table = dict(base)
scan_algebra.py:612:                table[(low, high)] = wrong
scan_algebra.py:613:                bad = add_with(a, b, lambda x, y, table=table: table[(x, y)])
scan_algebra.py:614:                changes = bad["sum"] != true_sum
scan_algebra.py:615:                crosses = not same_carry_class(base[(low, high)], wrong)
scan_algebra.py:616:                wrongs.append({
scan_algebra.py:617:                    "wrong": wrong, "sum": bad["sum"], "changes": changes, "crosses_carry_class": crosses,
scan_algebra.py:618:                })
scan_algebra.py:619:            out.append({
scan_algebra.py:620:                "low": low, "high": high, "a": a, "b": b, "true_sum": true_sum,
scan_algebra.py:621:                "wrongs": wrongs,
scan_algebra.py:622:                "every_carry_crossing_changes": all(w["changes"] for w in wrongs if w["crosses_carry_class"]),
scan_algebra.py:623:                "no_same_class_changes": all(not w["changes"] for w in wrongs if not w["crosses_carry_class"]),
scan_algebra.py:624:            })
scan_algebra.py:625:    return out

scan_algebra.py:662:        "seed_scheme": "random.Random(seed) then n draws of two n-digit numbers, arith_data.random_number rules",

scan_forward.py:92:def _rank_triples(table, ids, limit):
scan_forward.py:93:    """Triples with pairwise cosine <= 0.3, ordered by (max pairwise cosine, sorted token ids)."""
scan_forward.py:94:    n = len(ids)
scan_forward.py:95:    keys = []
scan_forward.py:96:    for i in range(n):
scan_forward.py:97:        row_i = table[i]
scan_forward.py:98:        for j in range(i + 1, n):
scan_forward.py:99:            cij = row_i[j]
scan_forward.py:100:            row_j = table[j]
scan_forward.py:101:            for k in range(j + 1, n):
scan_forward.py:102:                m = cij
scan_forward.py:103:                if row_i[k] > m:
scan_forward.py:104:                    m = row_i[k]
scan_forward.py:105:                if row_j[k] > m:
scan_forward.py:106:                    m = row_j[k]
scan_forward.py:107:                if m > COSINE_MAX:
scan_forward.py:108:                    continue
scan_forward.py:109:                trip = tuple(sorted((int(ids[i]), int(ids[j]), int(ids[k]))))
scan_forward.py:110:                keys.append((m, trip))
scan_forward.py:111:    keys.sort()
scan_forward.py:112:    return keys[:limit]
scan_forward.py:113:
scan_forward.py:114:
scan_forward.py:115:def _pool_ids(names, id_of, banned):
scan_forward.py:116:    out = []
scan_forward.py:117:    seen = set()
scan_forward.py:118:    for name in names:
scan_forward.py:119:        i = int(id_of[name])
scan_forward.py:120:        if i in banned or i in seen:
scan_forward.py:121:            continue
scan_forward.py:122:        seen.add(i)
scan_forward.py:123:        out.append(i)
scan_forward.py:124:    return out

scan_forward.py:127:def _ranked_from_model(model, tok, limit):
scan_forward.py:128:    """Cosine order inside the first Moore pool that has a legal triple. Test pool, then train pool."""
scan_forward.py:129:    import torch
scan_forward.py:130:    import pb_pin
scan_forward.py:131:    weight = model.get_input_embeddings().weight.detach()
scan_forward.py:132:    dig = digit_token_ids(tok)
scan_forward.py:133:    nl = newline_id(tok)
scan_forward.py:134:    banned = set(dig) | {nl}
scan_forward.py:135:    pool = pb_pin.name_pool(tok)
scan_forward.py:136:    for split in ("test", "train"):
scan_forward.py:137:        ids = _pool_ids(pool[split], pool["id_of"], banned)
scan_forward.py:138:        if len(ids) < 3:
scan_forward.py:139:            continue
scan_forward.py:140:        idx = torch.tensor(ids, device=weight.device)
scan_forward.py:141:        x = weight.index_select(0, idx).float()
scan_forward.py:142:        x = x / x.norm(dim=-1, keepdim=True).clamp(min=1e-8)
scan_forward.py:143:        table = (x @ x.T).cpu().tolist()
scan_forward.py:144:        del x
scan_forward.py:145:        ranked = _rank_triples(table, ids, limit)
scan_forward.py:146:        if ranked:
scan_forward.py:147:            return ranked, split, weight, dig, nl
scan_forward.py:148:    raise RuntimeError("no K/P/G triple with pairwise cosine <= 0.3 in the Moore name pools")

scan_forward.py:151:def _symbol_record(model, tok, weight, trip, pool, sort_key, dig, nl):
scan_forward.py:152:    """K, P, G are the sorted token ids. The sort key is the max pairwise cosine from the ranking matrix."""
scan_forward.py:153:    import torch
scan_forward.py:154:    ordered = list(trip)
scan_forward.py:155:    names = ("K", "P", "G")
scan_forward.py:156:    mapping = {name: ordered[i] for i, name in enumerate(names)}
scan_forward.py:157:    idx = torch.tensor(ordered, device=weight.device)
scan_forward.py:158:    s = weight.index_select(0, idx).float()
scan_forward.py:159:    s = s / s.norm(dim=-1, keepdim=True).clamp(min=1e-8)
scan_forward.py:160:    pair = (s @ s.T).cpu()
scan_forward.py:161:    d = weight.index_select(0, torch.tensor(dig, device=weight.device)).float()
scan_forward.py:162:    d = d / d.norm(dim=-1, keepdim=True).clamp(min=1e-8)
scan_forward.py:163:    to_digit = (s @ d.T).cpu()
scan_forward.py:164:    pairs = {
scan_forward.py:165:        "KP": float(pair[0, 1]),
scan_forward.py:166:        "KG": float(pair[0, 2]),
scan_forward.py:167:        "PG": float(pair[1, 2]),
scan_forward.py:168:    }
scan_forward.py:169:    return {
scan_forward.py:170:        "pool": pool,
scan_forward.py:171:        "max_pairwise_cosine": sort_key,
scan_forward.py:172:        "pairwise": pairs,
scan_forward.py:173:        "id": mapping,
scan_forward.py:174:        "text": {name: tok.decode([mapping[name]]) for name in names},
scan_forward.py:175:        "max_cosine_to_digit": {name: float(to_digit[i].max()) for i, name in enumerate(names)},
scan_forward.py:176:        "digit_ids": dig,
scan_forward.py:177:        "nl": nl,
scan_forward.py:178:        "tie_word_embeddings": bool(getattr(model.config, "tie_word_embeddings", False)),
scan_forward.py:179:        "cosine_max": COSINE_MAX,
scan_forward.py:180:    }

scan_forward.py:183:def choose_symbols(model, tok):
scan_forward.py:184:    """Lexicographically first legal triple. Smallest id is K, middle is P, largest is G."""
scan_forward.py:185:    ranked, split, weight, dig, nl = _ranked_from_model(model, tok, 1)
scan_forward.py:186:    sort_key, trip = ranked[0]
scan_forward.py:187:    return _symbol_record(model, tok, weight, trip, split, sort_key, dig, nl)

scan_forward.py:190:def symbols_from_ids(model, tok, ids):
scan_forward.py:191:    """Build K, P, G from three token ids. Refuses if any pairwise cosine exceeds the cap.
scan_forward.py:192:
scan_forward.py:193:    Ids are sorted, so the smallest is K. This is the triple a finished search already selected.
scan_forward.py:194:    """
scan_forward.py:195:    import torch
scan_forward.py:196:    import pb_pin
scan_forward.py:197:    trip = tuple(sorted(int(i) for i in ids))
scan_forward.py:198:    if len(trip) != 3 or len(set(trip)) != 3:
scan_forward.py:199:        raise RuntimeError(f"need 3 distinct token ids, got {ids}")
scan_forward.py:200:    dig = digit_token_ids(tok)
scan_forward.py:201:    nl = newline_id(tok)
scan_forward.py:202:    if set(trip) & (set(dig) | {nl}):
scan_forward.py:203:        raise RuntimeError("a symbol id collides with a digit or the newline")
scan_forward.py:204:    weight = model.get_input_embeddings().weight.detach()
scan_forward.py:205:    idx = torch.tensor(list(trip), device=weight.device)
scan_forward.py:206:    vec = weight.index_select(0, idx).float()
scan_forward.py:207:    vec = vec / vec.norm(dim=-1, keepdim=True).clamp(min=1e-8)
scan_forward.py:208:    pair = (vec @ vec.T).cpu()
scan_forward.py:209:    sort_key = max(float(pair[0, 1]), float(pair[0, 2]), float(pair[1, 2]))
scan_forward.py:210:    if sort_key > COSINE_MAX:
scan_forward.py:211:        raise RuntimeError(f"pairwise cosine {sort_key} exceeds {COSINE_MAX}")
scan_forward.py:212:    pool = pb_pin.name_pool(tok)
scan_forward.py:213:    id_to_split = {}
scan_forward.py:214:    for split in ("test", "train"):
scan_forward.py:215:        for name in pool[split]:
scan_forward.py:216:            id_to_split[int(pool["id_of"][name])] = split
scan_forward.py:217:    splits = {id_to_split.get(i) for i in trip}
scan_forward.py:218:    used = next(iter(splits)) if len(splits) == 1 and None not in splits else "pinned"
scan_forward.py:219:    return _symbol_record(model, tok, weight, trip, used, sort_key, dig, nl)

scan_forward.py:225:def _pi(rows, nl):
scan_forward.py:226:    pi = []

scan_forward.py:228:        pi.extend(row)
scan_forward.py:229:        pi.append(nl)

scan_forward.py:233:def build_tables(symbols):
scan_forward.py:234:    """D and M are Moore rows (left, right, out, newline). S reuses the adder's five-token rows.
scan_forward.py:235:
scan_forward.py:236:    The deployed S query is the one forward [c, a, b, c'] reading the sum digit, which is the pinned
scan_forward.py:237:    forward 2. c' is the carry the scan already computed, so S is one packed round.
scan_forward.py:238:    """
scan_forward.py:239:    dig = symbols["digit_ids"]
scan_forward.py:240:    sym = symbols["id"]
scan_forward.py:241:    nl = symbols["nl"]
scan_forward.py:242:    d_rows, d_expect = [], {}
scan_forward.py:243:    for a, b, out in alg.digit_rows():
scan_forward.py:244:        row = [dig[a], dig[b], sym[out]]
scan_forward.py:245:        d_rows.append(row)
scan_forward.py:246:        d_expect[(dig[a], dig[b])] = sym[out]
scan_forward.py:247:    m_rows, m_expect = [], {}
scan_forward.py:248:    for low, high, out in alg.monoid_rows():
scan_forward.py:249:        row = [sym[low], sym[high], sym[out]]
scan_forward.py:250:        m_rows.append(row)
scan_forward.py:251:        m_expect[(sym[low], sym[high])] = sym[out]
scan_forward.py:252:    s_rows, s_expect = [], {}
scan_forward.py:253:    for c, a, b, cout, s in alg.adder_rows():
scan_forward.py:254:        row = [dig[c], dig[a], dig[b], dig[cout], dig[s]]
scan_forward.py:255:        s_rows.append(row)
scan_forward.py:256:        s_expect[(dig[c], dig[a], dig[b], dig[cout])] = dig[s]
scan_forward.py:257:    def pack(rows, expect, block_len):
scan_forward.py:258:        return {"rows": rows, "expect": expect, "pi": _pi(rows, nl), "block_len": block_len,
scan_forward.py:259:                "queries": [row[:block_len] for row in rows]}
scan_forward.py:260:    # D and M queries are the first two tokens; S query is the first four (c a b c'), not the sum digit.
scan_forward.py:261:    return {
scan_forward.py:262:        "D": pack(d_rows, d_expect, 2),
scan_forward.py:263:        "M": pack(m_rows, m_expect, 2),
scan_forward.py:264:        "S": pack(s_rows, s_expect, 4),
scan_forward.py:265:        "symbols": symbols,
scan_forward.py:266:    }

scan_forward.py:269:def certify_b1(model, table, tok):
scan_forward.py:270:    """Every row, one packed block each, shared Π. Pass means argmax equals the row and the margin is positive."""
scan_forward.py:271:    past = prefill(model, table["pi"])
scan_forward.py:272:    summary, seconds = packed_lookup(model, past, len(table["pi"]), table["queries"])
scan_forward.py:273:    del past
scan_forward.py:274:    bl = table["block_len"]
scan_forward.py:275:    misses = []
scan_forward.py:276:    miss_keys = []
scan_forward.py:277:    min_margin = None
scan_forward.py:278:    n_ok = 0
scan_forward.py:279:    for i, ((arg, margin), row) in enumerate(zip(summary, table["rows"])):
scan_forward.py:280:        target = row[bl]
scan_forward.py:281:        if min_margin is None or margin < min_margin:
scan_forward.py:282:            min_margin = margin
scan_forward.py:283:        ok = arg == target and margin > 0
scan_forward.py:284:        n_ok += int(ok)
scan_forward.py:285:        if ok:
scan_forward.py:286:            continue
scan_forward.py:287:        query = row[:bl]
scan_forward.py:288:        query_text = tuple(tok.decode([int(t)]) for t in query)
scan_forward.py:289:        got_text = tok.decode([int(arg)])
scan_forward.py:290:        target_text = tok.decode([int(target)])
scan_forward.py:291:        miss_keys.append((i, query_text, got_text, target_text))
scan_forward.py:292:        if len(misses) < 4:
scan_forward.py:293:            misses.append({
scan_forward.py:294:                "i": i,
scan_forward.py:295:                "query": [int(t) for t in query],
scan_forward.py:296:                "query_text": list(query_text),
scan_forward.py:297:                "target": int(target),
scan_forward.py:298:                "target_text": target_text,
scan_forward.py:299:                "got": int(arg),
scan_forward.py:300:                "got_text": got_text,
scan_forward.py:301:                "margin": margin,
scan_forward.py:302:            })
scan_forward.py:303:    n = len(table["rows"])
scan_forward.py:304:    return {"n": n, "n_ok": n_ok, "n_miss": n - n_ok, "min_margin": min_margin, "seconds": seconds,
scan_forward.py:305:            "misses": misses, "miss_keys": miss_keys, "passed": n_ok == n}

scan_forward.py:308:def search_certified_symbols(model, tok, limit=SEARCH_LIMIT, on_checkpoint=None):
scan_forward.py:309:    """First triple in the cosine order whose D, M, and S rows all pass packed B=1.
scan_forward.py:310:
scan_forward.py:311:    Rank 0 is the triple `choose_symbols` returns. A triple that fails D is not asked about M.
scan_forward.py:312:    The walk stops at the first triple that passes D, M, S, and the full invariance certificate.
scan_forward.py:313:    `on_checkpoint` receives a partial trace every 200 triples so a killed job still leaves a record.
scan_forward.py:314:    """
scan_forward.py:315:    ranked, split, weight, dig, nl = _ranked_from_model(model, tok, limit)
scan_forward.py:316:    chosen = None
scan_forward.py:317:    stopped = "cap"
scan_forward.py:318:    chosen_rank = None
scan_forward.py:319:    rank0 = None
scan_forward.py:320:    d_passes = []
scan_forward.py:321:    signatures = {}
scan_forward.py:322:    k_counts = {}
scan_forward.py:323:    n_tried = 0
scan_forward.py:324:    t0 = time.perf_counter()
scan_forward.py:325:
scan_forward.py:326:    def snapshot(partial):
scan_forward.py:327:        signature_counts = [
scan_forward.py:328:            {"i": i, "query_text": list(query), "got_text": got, "target_text": target, "n": n}
scan_forward.py:329:            for (i, query, got, target), n in sorted(signatures.items(), key=lambda item: (-item[1], item[0][0]))
scan_forward.py:330:        ]
scan_forward.py:331:        rare = [item for item in signature_counts if item["n"] < 2]
scan_forward.py:332:        return {
scan_forward.py:333:            "limit": limit,
scan_forward.py:334:            "pool": split,
scan_forward.py:335:            "n_ranked": len(ranked),
scan_forward.py:336:            "n_tried": n_tried,
scan_forward.py:337:            "n_d_pass": len(d_passes),
scan_forward.py:338:            "n_pass": sum(1 for item in d_passes if item["passed"]),
scan_forward.py:339:            "chosen_rank": chosen_rank,
scan_forward.py:340:            "stopped_because": stopped,
scan_forward.py:341:            "cosine_max": COSINE_MAX,
scan_forward.py:342:            "seconds": time.perf_counter() - t0,
scan_forward.py:343:            "partial": partial,
scan_forward.py:344:            "rank0": rank0,
scan_forward.py:345:            "d_passes": d_passes,
scan_forward.py:346:            "signature_n_sum": sum(item["n"] for item in signature_counts),
scan_forward.py:347:            "signature_other_n": sum(item["n"] for item in rare),
scan_forward.py:348:            "signature_other_distinct": len(rare),

scan_forward.py:434:def corrupt_m(tables, low, high, wrong):
scan_forward.py:435:    """A copy of M whose one row (low, high) writes `wrong` instead of the true product."""
scan_forward.py:436:    sym = tables["symbols"]["id"]
scan_forward.py:437:    nl = tables["symbols"]["nl"]

scan_forward.py:439:    rows = [list(r) for r in src["rows"]]

scan_forward.py:441:    found = False
scan_forward.py:442:    for row in rows:
scan_forward.py:443:        if (row[0], row[1]) == key:
scan_forward.py:444:            row[2] = sym[wrong]
scan_forward.py:445:            found = True
scan_forward.py:446:    if not found:
scan_forward.py:447:        raise RuntimeError("row missing")
scan_forward.py:448:    expect = dict(src["expect"])

scan_forward.py:457:def allow_mask(P, n_blocks, block_len, device):
scan_forward.py:458:    """Bool mask, True = may attend, shape (1, 1, Q, P+Q). Matches alg.attention_allows."""
scan_forward.py:459:    import torch
scan_forward.py:460:    Q = n_blocks * block_len
scan_forward.py:461:    K = P + Q
scan_forward.py:462:    allow = torch.zeros((1, 1, Q, K), dtype=torch.bool, device=device)
scan_forward.py:463:    if Q == 0:
scan_forward.py:464:        return allow
scan_forward.py:465:    allow[:, :, :, :P] = True
scan_forward.py:466:    q = torch.arange(Q, device=device)
scan_forward.py:467:    k = torch.arange(Q, device=device)
scan_forward.py:468:    bq = torch.div(q, block_len, rounding_mode="floor")
scan_forward.py:469:    bk = torch.div(k, block_len, rounding_mode="floor")
scan_forward.py:470:    off_q = q - bq * block_len
scan_forward.py:471:    off_k = k - bk * block_len
scan_forward.py:472:    local = (bq[:, None] == bk[None, :]) & (off_k[None, :] <= off_q[:, None])
scan_forward.py:473:    allow[0, 0, :, P:] = local
scan_forward.py:474:    if Q * K <= 2_000_000:
scan_forward.py:475:        got = int(allow.sum().item())
scan_forward.py:476:        want = alg.allow_count(P, n_blocks, block_len)
scan_forward.py:477:        if got != want:
scan_forward.py:478:            raise RuntimeError(f"mask count {got} != {want} for P={P} B={n_blocks} block={block_len}")
scan_forward.py:479:    return allow

scan_forward.py:482:def prefill(model, pi_ids):
scan_forward.py:483:    import torch
scan_forward.py:484:    import pb_pin
scan_forward.py:485:    device = _device(model)
scan_forward.py:486:    ids = torch.tensor([pi_ids], device=device)
scan_forward.py:487:    pos = torch.arange(len(pi_ids), device=device)[None]
scan_forward.py:488:    with torch.inference_mode():
scan_forward.py:489:        past = model(input_ids=ids, position_ids=pos, use_cache=True).past_key_values
scan_forward.py:490:    if past.get_seq_length() != len(pi_ids):
scan_forward.py:491:        raise RuntimeError(f"prefill length {past.get_seq_length()} != {len(pi_ids)}")
scan_forward.py:492:    return past

scan_forward.py:495:def _summarize(ends):
scan_forward.py:496:    """ends: (B, V) bf16. Returns list of (argmax, margin) using float32 top-2, in chunks."""
scan_forward.py:497:    import torch
scan_forward.py:498:    out = []
scan_forward.py:499:    for i in range(0, ends.shape[0], 256):
scan_forward.py:500:        chunk = ends[i:i + 256].float()
scan_forward.py:501:        vals, idx = torch.topk(chunk, 2, dim=-1)
scan_forward.py:502:        for j in range(chunk.shape[0]):
scan_forward.py:503:            out.append((int(idx[j, 0]), float(vals[j, 0] - vals[j, 1])))
scan_forward.py:504:        del chunk
scan_forward.py:505:    return out

scan_forward.py:508:def _block_ends(logits, n_blocks, block_len):
scan_forward.py:509:    import torch
scan_forward.py:510:    idx = torch.arange(n_blocks, device=logits.device) * block_len + (block_len - 1)
scan_forward.py:511:    return logits.index_select(0, idx)

scan_forward.py:514:def packed_lookup(model, past, P, blocks):
scan_forward.py:515:    """One forward. `blocks` share `past` (cloned here, so Π is not written). Logits at each block's last token."""
scan_forward.py:516:    import torch
scan_forward.py:517:    import pb_pin
scan_forward.py:518:    if not blocks:
scan_forward.py:519:        return [], 0.0
scan_forward.py:520:    block_len = len(blocks[0])
scan_forward.py:521:    if any(len(b) != block_len for b in blocks):
scan_forward.py:522:        raise RuntimeError("mixed block lengths")
scan_forward.py:523:    device = _device(model)
scan_forward.py:524:    ids = [t for b in blocks for t in b]
scan_forward.py:525:    pos = []
scan_forward.py:526:    for _ in blocks:
scan_forward.py:527:        pos.extend(range(P, P + block_len))
scan_forward.py:528:    mask = allow_mask(P, len(blocks), block_len, device)
scan_forward.py:529:    cache = pb_pin._clone(past)
scan_forward.py:530:    before = past.get_seq_length()
scan_forward.py:531:    input_ids = torch.tensor([ids], device=device)
scan_forward.py:532:    position_ids = torch.tensor([pos], device=device)
scan_forward.py:533:    _sync()
scan_forward.py:534:    t0 = time.perf_counter()
scan_forward.py:535:    with torch.inference_mode():
scan_forward.py:536:        out = model(input_ids=input_ids, position_ids=position_ids, attention_mask=mask,
scan_forward.py:537:                    past_key_values=cache, use_cache=True)
scan_forward.py:538:    _sync()
scan_forward.py:539:    seconds = time.perf_counter() - t0
scan_forward.py:540:    if past.get_seq_length() != before:
scan_forward.py:541:        raise RuntimeError("packed forward wrote the shared Π cache")
scan_forward.py:542:    ends = _block_ends(out.logits[0], len(blocks), block_len)
scan_forward.py:543:    summary = _summarize(ends)
scan_forward.py:544:    del out, cache, ends
scan_forward.py:545:    return summary, seconds

scan_forward.py:602:    import torch

scan_forward.py:645:def _absorb(report, equal, delta, margin, argmax_ok):
scan_forward.py:646:    report["n_compared"] += 1
scan_forward.py:647:    report["n_equal"] += int(equal and delta == 0.0)
scan_forward.py:648:    if delta > report["max_abs_delta"]:
scan_forward.py:649:        report["max_abs_delta"] = delta
scan_forward.py:650:    if margin is not None:
scan_forward.py:651:        report["min_margin"] = margin if report["min_margin"] is None else min(report["min_margin"], margin)
scan_forward.py:652:    if not argmax_ok:
scan_forward.py:653:        report["n_argmax_mismatch"] += 1

scan_forward.py:656:def _top(logit):
scan_forward.py:657:    import torch
scan_forward.py:658:    vals, idx = torch.topk(logit.float(), 2)
scan_forward.py:659:    return int(idx[0]), float(vals[0] - vals[1])

scan_forward.py:662:def _absorb_ends(report, ends, ref, target):
scan_forward.py:663:    """Every slot of `ends` against one reference row. bf16->float32 is exact, so a 0 delta is bitwise equality."""
scan_forward.py:664:    import torch
scan_forward.py:665:    fails = 0
scan_forward.py:666:    for i in range(0, ends.shape[0], 128):
scan_forward.py:667:        part = ends[i:i + 128].float()
scan_forward.py:668:        vals, idx = torch.topk(part, 2, dim=-1)
scan_forward.py:669:        delta = (part - ref.float()).abs().amax(dim=-1)
scan_forward.py:670:        for j in range(part.shape[0]):
scan_forward.py:671:            arg = int(idx[j, 0])
scan_forward.py:672:            margin = float(vals[j, 0] - vals[j, 1])
scan_forward.py:673:            d = float(delta[j])
scan_forward.py:674:            ok = arg == target and margin > 0
scan_forward.py:675:            _absorb(report, d == 0.0, d, margin, ok)
scan_forward.py:676:            fails += int(not ok)
scan_forward.py:677:        del part
scan_forward.py:678:    return fails

scan_forward.py:681:def measure_invariance(model, tables, widths=None, row_limit=None, include_chunk=True):
scan_forward.py:682:    """Largest |Δlogit| across slot, width B, and packed-versus-batched, against the pinned single tick.
scan_forward.py:683:
scan_forward.py:684:    Certifies every touched slot: argmax equals the row and the margin is positive. `row_limit` keeps a smoke
scan_forward.py:685:    run small; None means every row.
scan_forward.py:686:    """
scan_forward.py:687:    report = {"tables": {}, "n_compared": 0, "n_equal": 0, "max_abs_delta": 0.0, "min_margin": None,
scan_forward.py:688:              "n_argmax_mismatch": 0, "widths": None}
scan_forward.py:689:    for name in ("D", "M", "S"):
scan_forward.py:690:        table = tables[name]
scan_forward.py:691:        past = prefill(model, table["pi"])
scan_forward.py:692:        P = len(table["pi"])
scan_forward.py:693:        queries = table["queries"]
scan_forward.py:694:        bl = table["block_len"]
scan_forward.py:695:        chunk = chunk_for(bl)
scan_forward.py:696:        use = queries if row_limit is None else queries[:row_limit]
scan_forward.py:697:        targets = [q_row[bl] for q_row in (table["rows"] if row_limit is None else table["rows"][:row_limit])]
scan_forward.py:698:        # targets: D/M row[2] and the query is row[:2], so row[bl] is the output. S row[4] with bl=4.
scan_forward.py:699:        refs = [single_lookup(model, past, P, q) for q in use]
scan_forward.py:700:        widths_here = list(widths or (1, 8, 32))
scan_forward.py:701:        if include_chunk and chunk not in widths_here:
scan_forward.py:702:            widths_here.append(chunk)
scan_forward.py:703:        table_rec = {"rows": len(use), "P": P, "chunk": chunk, "widths": widths_here,
scan_forward.py:704:                     "b1_argmax_bad": 0, "cert_fail": 0}
scan_forward.py:705:        for b in widths_here:
scan_forward.py:706:            # every slot of an all-equal forward, for every row, then one mixed forward
scan_forward.py:707:            for i, (q, target) in enumerate(zip(use, targets)):
scan_forward.py:708:                ends, _ = packed_end_logits(model, past, P, [q] * b)
scan_forward.py:709:                fails = _absorb_ends(report, ends, refs[i], target)
scan_forward.py:710:                table_rec["cert_fail"] += fails
scan_forward.py:711:                if b == 1:
scan_forward.py:712:                    table_rec["b1_argmax_bad"] += fails
scan_forward.py:713:                del ends
scan_forward.py:714:            mixed_blocks = [use[i % len(use)] for i in range(b)]
scan_forward.py:715:            ends, _ = packed_end_logits(model, past, P, mixed_blocks)
scan_forward.py:716:            for s in range(0, b, 128):
scan_forward.py:717:                part = ends[s:s + 128]
scan_forward.py:718:                for j in range(part.shape[0]):
scan_forward.py:719:                    i = (s + j) % len(use)
scan_forward.py:720:                    arg, margin = _top(part[j])
scan_forward.py:721:                    equal, delta = _logit_delta(part[j], refs[i])
scan_forward.py:722:                    ok = arg == targets[i] and margin > 0
scan_forward.py:723:                    _absorb(report, equal, delta, margin, ok)
scan_forward.py:724:                    table_rec["cert_fail"] += int(not ok)
scan_forward.py:725:            del ends
scan_forward.py:726:        # batched reference, Π repeated, small width so the repeated prefix stays cheap
scan_forward.py:727:        batch_w = min(4, len(use))
scan_forward.py:728:        for start in range(0, len(use), batch_w):
scan_forward.py:729:            group = use[start:start + batch_w]
scan_forward.py:730:            import torch
scan_forward.py:731:            device = _device(model)
scan_forward.py:732:            rows = [table["pi"] + list(q) for q in group]
scan_forward.py:733:            pos = [list(range(P + bl)) for _ in group]
scan_forward.py:734:            with torch.inference_mode():
scan_forward.py:735:                out = model(input_ids=torch.tensor(rows, device=device),
scan_forward.py:736:                            position_ids=torch.tensor(pos, device=device), use_cache=False)
scan_forward.py:737:            for j, q in enumerate(group):
scan_forward.py:738:                arg, margin = _top(out.logits[j, -1])
scan_forward.py:739:                equal, delta = _logit_delta(out.logits[j, -1], refs[start + j])
scan_forward.py:740:                ok = arg == targets[start + j] and margin > 0
scan_forward.py:741:                _absorb(report, equal, delta, margin, ok)
scan_forward.py:742:                table_rec["cert_fail"] += int(not ok)
scan_forward.py:743:            del out
scan_forward.py:744:        table_rec["pass"] = table_rec["cert_fail"] == 0 and table_rec["b1_argmax_bad"] == 0
scan_forward.py:745:        report["tables"][name] = table_rec
scan_forward.py:746:        del refs, past
scan_forward.py:747:        torch_empty()
scan_forward.py:748:    report["include_chunk"] = include_chunk
scan_forward.py:749:    report["bitwise"] = report["n_compared"] > 0 and report["n_equal"] == report["n_compared"] and report["max_abs_delta"] == 0.0
scan_forward.py:750:    bound = 2.0 * report["max_abs_delta"]
scan_forward.py:751:    report["margin_bound"] = bound
scan_forward.py:752:    report["margin_ok"] = report["min_margin"] is not None and report["min_margin"] > bound
scan_forward.py:753:    if report["bitwise"]:
scan_forward.py:754:        report["decision"] = "bitwise"
scan_forward.py:755:        report["empirical_bound"] = False
scan_forward.py:756:        report["decision_ok"] = True
scan_forward.py:757:    else:
scan_forward.py:758:        report["decision"] = "margin"
scan_forward.py:759:        report["empirical_bound"] = True
scan_forward.py:760:        report["decision_ok"] = bool(report["margin_ok"]) and report["n_argmax_mismatch"] == 0
scan_forward.py:761:    return report

scan_forward.py:772:def _consume(summary, expect, stats):

scan_forward.py:791:def run_schedule(model, past, values, rounds, original, identity_id, expect, stats, chunk):
scan_forward.py:792:    """`expect` maps (low_id, high_id) to the output id Π says. The array follows the model's argmax."""
scan_forward.py:793:    a = list(values)
scan_forward.py:794:    original = list(original)
scan_forward.py:795:    P = past.get_seq_length()

scan_forward.py:800:        if lookups:

scan_forward.py:802:            summary, pieces = chunked_packed(model, past, P, blocks, chunk)
scan_forward.py:803:            _add_seconds(stats, pieces)

scan_forward.py:805:        li = 0

scan_forward.py:809:                arg, margin = summary[li]
scan_forward.py:810:                li += 1

scan_forward.py:812:                match = target is not None and arg == target and margin > 0
scan_forward.py:813:                if target is None:
scan_forward.py:814:                    stats["n_missing"] += 1
scan_forward.py:815:                if not match and len(stats["bad"]) < 8:

scan_forward.py:819:            elif act[0] == "value":
scan_forward.py:820:                _, dest, value = act
scan_forward.py:821:                new[dest] = value
scan_forward.py:822:            elif act[0] == "identity":
scan_forward.py:823:                new[act[1]] = identity_id
scan_forward.py:824:        a = new
scan_forward.py:825:    return a

SCAN.md:3:## Summary
SCAN.md:4:
SCAN.md:5:Savant r3 adds by a certified carry-lookahead scan over the {K, P, G} monoid. 2 certification records, 1 with decision_ok. The last is margin (decision_ok True, max |Δlogit| 0.5, min margin 1.5). At 4096 digits, 16 of 16 additions matched bigint on both schedules with every lookup on its row. At 100000 digits, 4 of 4 matched. Hillis–Steele model seconds: 115.676, 115.024, 115.223, 114.895. The L4 4096-digit addition has hs_exact True and bl_exact True, Hillis–Steele model seconds 34.5097. At 1000000 digits, hs_exact True, bl_exact True, hs_rounds 22, Hillis–Steele model seconds 1343.35. Settled ledger estimate 10.2727 USD against a cap of 15 USD.
SCAN.md:6:
SCAN.md:7:## Resolved choices
SCAN.md:8:
SCAN.md:9:These are fixed so the runs have one meaning. None of them is a stop-question under the brief's rule 9.
SCAN.md:10:
SCAN.md:11:- n columns for two n-digit operands; final carry is a leading digit, no extra 0 0 column.
SCAN.md:12:- D and M are Moore rows: left token, right token, output token, newline. The query is the first two tokens. S reuses the certified adder's five-token rows. The query is the four-token block [carry-in, a, b, carry-out], and the argmax at the last position is the sum digit. The carry-out in that query is the adder row's own carry-out for that carry-in and those digits, so the query addresses a row that exists. On a correct monoid it equals the scan prefix's carry. The record hs_carry_matches_adder is that check.
SCAN.md:13:- The deployed path prefills Π once and then runs packed blocks on a clone of that cache. Positions restart at |Π| inside each block. The mask is block-diagonal. The batched path, with Π repeated in every row, is the reference used in the invariance grid.
SCAN.md:14:- Each Light triple is four model lookups: the two inner products, then the two outer ones. 18 corruptions times 27 triples is 1944 lookups. The brief's parenthetical counted triples.
SCAN.md:15:- State tokens walk the Moore name pool in cosine order: pairwise cosine at most 0.3, smallest maximum first, then sorted token ids. K, P, and G are those ids in increasing order. The deployed triple is the first one whose invariance certificate passes. The symbol-selection section records that rank.
SCAN.md:16:
SCAN.md:17:Closeout record `research/closeout/results/rt-arith-gate/rt-gate_arith.json` has args.seed 777, 64 kept records, lengths [10, 100, 1000, 4096], and 16 records whose L field is 4096. The longest stored question is 300 characters. Two 4096-digit operands do not fit in that question, and the scan draw uses seed 20261028. The sequential-clock comparison is not applicable. No new sequential run was paid for. The bigint check is the check that was run.
SCAN.md:18:
SCAN.md:19:## Registered predictions
SCAN.md:20:
SCAN.md:21:Registered before any Modal spend. Every count below was written by scan_report.py from results/algebra.json (scan_algebra.audit, seed 20261028).
SCAN.md:22:
SCAN.md:23:1. Every row of D (100), M (9) and S (200) certifies on the packed path: the argmax equals the row and the margin is positive. The bitwise-or-margin rule is fixed here and is not a predicted branch. If the invariance grid is bitwise equal (the maximum absolute logit difference is 0), the certificate is bitwise. Otherwise the certificate is a margin certificate, and it passes only when the minimum packed margin is greater than twice that maximum absolute difference. A margin certificate is an empirical bound.
SCAN.md:24:
SCAN.md:25:2. On seed 20261028, 16 of 16 additions of 4096 digits and 4 of 4 additions of 100000 digits are exact under both Hillis–Steele and Blelloch. Exact means the digit string equals Python's bigint sum, the two schedules agree on every carry, and every lookup's argmax equals its row. When the certificate is a margin certificate, every lookup's margin also clears the bound from item 1.
SCAN.md:26:
SCAN.md:27:3. Hillis–Steele uses ceil(log2 n)+2 model rounds, counting the D round and the S round. That is 14 rounds at 4096 digits, 19 at 100000 digits, and 22 at 1000000 digits.
SCAN.md:28:
SCAN.md:29:4. One Hillis–Steele addition of 100000 digits takes under 10 seconds of model time on an H100. Model time is the packed forwards of D, the Hillis–Steele lookups, and S. It excludes the merge, the certification grid, Blelloch, and the fault injection.
SCAN.md:30:
SCAN.md:31:5. The in-model Light test flags exactly the 14 corruptions whose stored tables fail Light's test, and none of the 4 it does not flag (KP->G, KG->K, GK->G, GP->K). The planted row is KK->G. Its failing triples are KKK, KGK.
SCAN.md:32:
SCAN.md:33:6. The three state tokens chosen for K, P and G have pairwise cosine at most 0.3.
SCAN.md:34:
SCAN.md:35:The 1000000-digit addition is attempted only if the settled ledger still has room under the $15 cap after the 100000-digit run. There is no prediction that it will be run.
SCAN.md:36:
SCAN.md:37:Closeout's sequential clock is not a comparison target. The reason is computed in the resolved-choices section from the closeout record on disk.
SCAN.md:38:
SCAN.md:39:## Amendments

SCAN.md:433:Wrong sum digits are the columns whose carry-in differs, plus the leading digit when the final carry differs. They are not the columns where the corrupted row was read. A use writes a prefix, and the following columns stay wrong through later P blocks until a K or G is absorbed. The counts above are that closure, recomputed from the saved operands.
SCAN.md:434:
SCAN.md:435:## Theory
SCAN.md:436:
SCAN.md:437:The carrier is {K, P, G}. D sends a digit pair (a, b) to K when a+b ≤ 8, P when a+b = 9, and G when a+b ≥ 10. M is the binary operation x ⊕ y = y when y is not P, and x when y is P. P is a two-sided identity: x ⊕ P = x and P ⊕ x = x. S is the ordinary sum digit of (carry-in, a, b).
SCAN.md:438:
SCAN.md:439:Associativity of M. Let x, y, z be any of the three symbols. If z is not P, then (x ⊕ y) ⊕ z = z and x ⊕ (y ⊕ z) = x ⊕ z = z. If z is P, then (x ⊕ y) ⊕ z = x ⊕ y and x ⊕ (y ⊕ z) = x ⊕ y. The two sides agree in every case, so every parenthesization of a word agrees. Light's test on the true table finds 0 failing triples.
SCAN.md:440:
SCAN.md:441:Carry. With the carry into a block equal to 0, the carry out is 1 exactly when the block is G. One column: the column is G exactly when a+b ≥ 10, which is exactly when the carry out is 1 and the carry in was 0. Several columns: write the word as a low block followed by a high block. If the high block is K or G, the product is the high block, so the carry out is the high block's own carry out, which does not depend on the carry into the low block. That is what K and G mean. If the high block is P, the product is the low block, so the carry out equals the low block's carry out, which is the carry that P lets through. By induction on the word, the carry out of a prefix is 1 exactly when the prefix is G, provided the carry into the whole sum is 0.
SCAN.md:442:
SCAN.md:443:Theorem P², sufficiency. Suppose every row of D, M and S is certified on the path that the scan actually runs, and M is the associative operation above. D assigns each column its true block. Any evaluation tree of M, including Hillis–Steele and Blelloch, computes the same prefixes, because every parenthesization agrees. The carry into each column is then the true carry. S, given that carry, the two digits, and the carry out that those three determine, returns the true sum digit. The final carry is the leading digit. So every bracketing and every schedule returns the exact sum.
SCAN.md:444:
SCAN.md:445:Necessity, corrected. Each of the 9 rows is the unique lookup in a 2-column input (necessity_all_rows_used True). A wrong output changes some sum exactly when it leaves the carry class of the true output. G is its own class; K and P both emit carry 0. There are 5 same-class corruptions (KK->P, KP->P, PK->P, PP->K, GK->P) and 13 carry-crossing corruptions (KK->G, KP->G, KG->K, KG->P, PK->G, PP->G, PG->K, PG->P, GK->G, GP->K, GP->P, GG->K, GG->P). necessity_carry_crossing_changes_sum True. necessity_same_carry_class_preserves_sum True. A K↔P error does not change any sum. Light's test is a different predicate: 4 corruptions preserve associativity (KP->G, KG->K, GK->G, GP->K), and of those the ones that still change a sum are the carry-crossing ones (KP->G, KG->K, GK->G, GP->K). An uncertified wrong row makes some input wrong when the wrong symbol crosses the carry class. It does not, when the wrong symbol stays in the class.
SCAN.md:446:
SCAN.md:447:Depth. The last prefix depends on every column. A lookup reads two earlier values, so a circuit of fan-in 2 has depth at least ceil(log2 n). Hillis–Steele meets that bound: 12 scan rounds at 4096 digits, 17 at 100000, 20 at 1000000, plus one D round and one S round. Blelloch uses about twice as many rounds and fewer lookups. Barrington (1989) showed that width-5 branching programs compute NC¹, and that the word problem of S5 is NC¹-complete. Log depth is therefore optimal for general automata unless TC⁰ = NC¹. That theorem is Barrington's. The bound claimed here is only the fan-in-2 bound for this 3-element monoid.
SCAN.md:448:
SCAN.md:449:## What could be wrong
SCAN.md:450:
SCAN.md:451:1. A batched kernel moves a logit across a decision boundary. The invariance grid's decision is margin, empirical_bound True, max |Δlogit| 0.5, min margin 1.5, twice the max difference 1, decision_ok True, argmax mismatches 0. The margin form is an empirical bound.
SCAN.md:452:2. Low and high are swapped. 22 of 22 collected additions match Python's bigint sum on both schedules. A swapped combine is a different operation and does not reproduce those sums. The algebra tests check the same operand order against the sequential prefix.
SCAN.md:453:3. The K, P, G embeddings have cosine above 0.3. The merged model selected {'G': ' equation', 'K': ' knew', 'P': ' resp'} with maximum pairwise cosine 0.0208387, threshold 0.3, pairs KG 0.0208387, KP 0.00428171, PG 0.00810981.
SCAN.md:454:
SCAN.md:455:## Second derivation
SCAN.md:456:
SCAN.md:457:Detectable corruptions. algebra.json n_detectable is 14; n_detectable_rederived is 14; the count of stored fail-lists that are non-empty is 14; Light's test rerun on the stored 9-entry tables flags 14 (KK->P, KK->G, KP->P, KG->P, PK->P, PK->G, PP->K, PP->G, PG->K, PG->P, GK->P, GP->P, GG->K, GG->P). These four counts match: True.
SCAN.md:458:
SCAN.md:459:4096-digit exact count. 16 records. The files' hs_exact and bl_exact flags count 16. Re-adding the saved operand strings with Python int counts 16. The two counts match: True.
SCAN.md:460:
SCAN.md:461:Model seconds and Hillis–Steele rounds, on the 100000-digit records (4 files). Summing each file's chunk_seconds equals its model_seconds within 0.001 s, and hs_rounds equals ceil(log2 n)+2 from the recorded n_digits: True.

SCAN.md:463:All three second derivations match: True.

scan_report.py:601:def theory_text(algebra):
scan_report.py:602:    same, cross = carry_split(algebra)
scan_report.py:603:    undet_cross = [name for name in algebra["undetectable"] if name in cross]
scan_report.py:604:    sizes = algebra["sizes"]

scan_report.py:606:        "## Theory",
scan_report.py:607:        "",
scan_report.py:608:        "The carrier is {K, P, G}. D sends a digit pair (a, b) to K when a+b ≤ 8, P when a+b = 9, and G when "
scan_report.py:609:        "a+b ≥ 10. M is the binary operation x ⊕ y = y when y is not P, and x when y is P. P is a two-sided "
scan_report.py:610:        "identity: x ⊕ P = x and P ⊕ x = x. S is the ordinary sum digit of (carry-in, a, b).",
scan_report.py:611:        "",
scan_report.py:612:        "Associativity of M. Let x, y, z be any of the three symbols. If z is not P, then (x ⊕ y) ⊕ z = z and "
scan_report.py:613:        "x ⊕ (y ⊕ z) = x ⊕ z = z. If z is P, then (x ⊕ y) ⊕ z = x ⊕ y and x ⊕ (y ⊕ z) = x ⊕ y. The two sides "
scan_report.py:614:        f"agree in every case, so every parenthesization of a word agrees. Light's test on the true table finds "
scan_report.py:615:        f"{len(alg.light_failures(alg.combine))} failing triples.",
scan_report.py:616:        "",
scan_report.py:617:        "Carry. With the carry into a block equal to 0, the carry out is 1 exactly when the block is G. "
scan_report.py:618:        "One column: the column is G exactly when a+b ≥ 10, which is exactly when the carry out is 1 and the "
scan_report.py:619:        "carry in was 0. Several columns: write the word as a low block followed by a high block. If the high "
scan_report.py:620:        "block is K or G, the product is the high block, so the carry out is the high block's own carry out, "
scan_report.py:621:        "which does not depend on the carry into the low block. That is what K and G mean. If the high block "
scan_report.py:622:        "is P, the product is the low block, so the carry out equals the low block's carry out, which is the "
scan_report.py:623:        "carry that P lets through. By induction on the word, the carry out of a prefix is 1 exactly when the "
scan_report.py:624:        "prefix is G, provided the carry into the whole sum is 0.",
scan_report.py:625:        "",
scan_report.py:626:        "Theorem P², sufficiency. Suppose every row of D, M and S is certified on the path that the scan "
scan_report.py:627:        "actually runs, and M is the associative operation above. D assigns each column its true block. Any "
scan_report.py:628:        "evaluation tree of M, including Hillis–Steele and Blelloch, computes the same prefixes, because every "
scan_report.py:629:        "parenthesization agrees. The carry into each column is then the true carry. S, given that carry, the "
scan_report.py:630:        "two digits, and the carry out that those three determine, returns the true sum digit. The final carry "
scan_report.py:631:        "is the leading digit. So every bracketing and every schedule returns the exact sum.",
scan_report.py:632:        "",
scan_report.py:633:        "Necessity, corrected. Each of the 9 rows is the unique lookup in a 2-column input "
scan_report.py:634:        f"(necessity_all_rows_used {algebra['necessity_all_rows_used']}). A wrong output changes some sum "
scan_report.py:635:        "exactly when it leaves the carry class of the true output. G is its own class; K and P both emit "
scan_report.py:636:        f"carry 0. There are {len(same)} same-class corruptions ({', '.join(same)}) and {len(cross)} "
scan_report.py:637:        f"carry-crossing corruptions ({', '.join(cross)}). "
scan_report.py:638:        f"necessity_carry_crossing_changes_sum {algebra['necessity_carry_crossing_changes_sum']}. "
scan_report.py:639:        f"necessity_same_carry_class_preserves_sum {algebra['necessity_same_carry_class_preserves_sum']}. "
scan_report.py:640:        "A K↔P error does not change any sum. Light's test is a different predicate: "
scan_report.py:641:        f"{algebra['n_undetectable']} corruptions preserve associativity ({', '.join(algebra['undetectable'])}), "
scan_report.py:642:        f"and of those the ones that still change a sum are the carry-crossing ones ({', '.join(undet_cross) or 'none'}). "
scan_report.py:643:        "An uncertified wrong row makes some input wrong when the wrong symbol crosses the carry class. "
scan_report.py:644:        "It does not, when the wrong symbol stays in the class.",
scan_report.py:645:        "",
scan_report.py:646:        "Depth. The last prefix depends on every column. A lookup reads two earlier values, so a circuit of "
scan_report.py:647:        f"fan-in 2 has depth at least ceil(log2 n). Hillis–Steele meets that bound: "
scan_report.py:648:        f"{sizes['4096']['hs_scan_rounds']} scan rounds at 4096 digits, "
scan_report.py:649:        f"{sizes['100000']['hs_scan_rounds']} at 100000, {sizes['1000000']['hs_scan_rounds']} at 1000000, "
scan_report.py:650:        "plus one D round and one S round. Blelloch uses about twice as many rounds and fewer lookups. "
scan_report.py:651:        "Barrington (1989) showed that width-5 branching programs compute NC¹, and that the word problem of "
scan_report.py:652:        "S5 is NC¹-complete. Log depth is therefore optimal for general automata unless TC⁰ = NC¹. That "
scan_report.py:653:        "theorem is Barrington's. The bound claimed here is only the fan-in-2 bound for this 3-element monoid.",
scan_report.py:654:        "",

   420	### Corrupted 4096-digit sum
   421	
   422	`scan-counted2-h100/demo.json`, seed 20261028, host NVIDIA H100 80GB HBM3; unknown; GenuineIntel 6 143 unknown; avx512f=True, n_digits 4096, instance 0. Ledger gpu NVIDIA H100 80GB HBM3, cpu GenuineIntel | 6 | 143 | unknown | avx512f=True. Planted row KK->G (true output K).
   423	Model Hillis–Steele sum equals the algebra schedule: True. Model Blelloch sum equals the algebra schedule: True. The file's own flags were True and True. Both differ from the bigint sum: True. The two schedules differ from each other: True.
   424	- BL: n 12286, n_match 12286, min_margin 2.25.
   425	- D: n 4096, n_match 4096, min_margin 1.5.
   426	- HS: n 45057, n_match 45057, min_margin 2.25.
   427	- S_BL: n 4096, n_match 4096, min_margin 9.375.
   428	- S_HS: n 4096, n_match 4096, min_margin 9.375.
   429	Lookups matched the corrupted prefix: True. Recomputed propagation equals the stored where-record: True.
   430	- seq: n_used 595, n_cin_diff 668, every wrong column is strictly after a use True, every use poisons the next column True, wrong columns beyond that next column 73, cin_diff equals the use columns False, final carry differs False.
   431	- hs: n_used 1726, n_cin_diff 1734, every wrong column is strictly after a use True, every use poisons the next column True, wrong columns beyond that next column 8, cin_diff equals the use columns False, final carry differs False.
   432	- bl: n_used 1305, n_cin_diff 606, every wrong column is strictly after a use True, every use poisons the next column False, wrong columns beyond that next column 65, cin_diff equals the use columns False, final carry differs False.
   433	Wrong sum digits are the columns whose carry-in differs, plus the leading digit when the final carry differs. They are not the columns where the corrupted row was read. A use writes a prefix, and the following columns stay wrong through later P blocks until a K or G is absorbed. The counts above are that closure, recomputed from the saved operands.
   434	
   435	## Theory
   436	
   437	The carrier is {K, P, G}. D sends a digit pair (a, b) to K when a+b ≤ 8, P when a+b = 9, and G when a+b ≥ 10. M is the binary operation x ⊕ y = y when y is not P, and x when y is P. P is a two-sided identity: x ⊕ P = x and P ⊕ x = x. S is the ordinary sum digit of (carry-in, a, b).
   438	
   439	Associativity of M. Let x, y, z be any of the three symbols. If z is not P, then (x ⊕ y) ⊕ z = z and x ⊕ (y ⊕ z) = x ⊕ z = z. If z is P, then (x ⊕ y) ⊕ z = x ⊕ y and x ⊕ (y ⊕ z) = x ⊕ y. The two sides agree in every case, so every parenthesization of a word agrees. Light's test on the true table finds 0 failing triples.
   440	
   441	Carry. With the carry into a block equal to 0, the carry out is 1 exactly when the block is G. One column: the column is G exactly when a+b ≥ 10, which is exactly when the carry out is 1 and the carry in was 0. Several columns: write the word as a low block followed by a high block. If the high block is K or G, the product is the high block, so the carry out is the high block's own carry out, which does not depend on the carry into the low block. That is what K and G mean. If the high block is P, the product is the low block, so the carry out equals the low block's carry out, which is the carry that P lets through. By induction on the word, the carry out of a prefix is 1 exactly when the prefix is G, provided the carry into the whole sum is 0.
   442	
   443	Theorem P², sufficiency. Suppose every row of D, M and S is certified on the path that the scan actually runs, and M is the associative operation above. D assigns each column its true block. Any evaluation tree of M, including Hillis–Steele and Blelloch, computes the same prefixes, because every parenthesization agrees. The carry into each column is then the true carry. S, given that carry, the two digits, and the carry out that those three determine, returns the true sum digit. The final carry is the leading digit. So every bracketing and every schedule returns the exact sum.
   444	
   445	Necessity, corrected. Each of the 9 rows is the unique lookup in a 2-column input (necessity_all_rows_used True). A wrong output changes some sum exactly when it leaves the carry class of the true output. G is its own class; K and P both emit carry 0. There are 5 same-class corruptions (KK->P, KP->P, PK->P, PP->K, GK->P) and 13 carry-crossing corruptions (KK->G, KP->G, KG->K, KG->P, PK->G, PP->G, PG->K, PG->P, GK->G, GP->K, GP->P, GG->K, GG->P). necessity_carry_crossing_changes_sum True. necessity_same_carry_class_preserves_sum True. A K↔P error does not change any sum. Light's test is a different predicate: 4 corruptions preserve associativity (KP->G, KG->K, GK->G, GP->K), and of those the ones that still change a sum are the carry-crossing ones (KP->G, KG->K, GK->G, GP->K). An uncertified wrong row makes some input wrong when the wrong symbol crosses the carry class. It does not, when the wrong symbol stays in the class.
   446	
   447	Depth. The last prefix depends on every column. A lookup reads two earlier values, so a circuit of fan-in 2 has depth at least ceil(log2 n). Hillis–Steele meets that bound: 12 scan rounds at 4096 digits, 17 at 100000, 20 at 1000000, plus one D round and one S round. Blelloch uses about twice as many rounds and fewer lookups. Barrington (1989) showed that width-5 branching programs compute NC¹, and that the word problem of S5 is NC¹-complete. Log depth is therefore optimal for general automata unless TC⁰ = NC¹. That theorem is Barrington's. The bound claimed here is only the fan-in-2 bound for this 3-element monoid.
   448	
   449	## What could be wrong
   450	
   451	1. A batched kernel moves a logit across a decision boundary. The invariance grid's decision is margin, empirical_bound True, max |Δlogit| 0.5, min margin 1.5, twice the max difference 1, decision_ok True, argmax mismatches 0. The margin form is an empirical bound.
   452	2. Low and high are swapped. 22 of 22 collected additions match Python's bigint sum on both schedules. A swapped combine is a different operation and does not reproduce those sums. The algebra tests check the same operand order against the sequential prefix.
   453	3. The K, P, G embeddings have cosine above 0.3. The merged model selected {'G': ' equation', 'K': ' knew', 'P': ' resp'} with maximum pairwise cosine 0.0208387, threshold 0.3, pairs KG 0.0208387, KP 0.00428171, PG 0.00810981.
   454	
   455	## Second derivation

These four counts match: True.
   458	
   459	4096-digit exact count. 16 records. The files' hs_exact and bl_exact flags count 16. Re-adding the saved operand strings with Python int counts 16. The two counts match: True.
   460
