     1	# Packed lookups for a browser
     2	
     3	No site change ships with this. A browser that wants the same scan has to run the packed forward itself.
     4	
     5	The cached Qwen/Qwen3-4B config agrees with the job record on ('n_layers', 'n_kv_heads', 'head_dim', 'hidden', 'vocab'): True.
     6	
     7	Architecture source: the job record. Layers 36, query heads 32, KV heads 8, head dim 128, hidden 2560, vocab 151936, dtype bfloat16, sliding window False.
     8	
     9	KV bytes per token are layers × 2 (key and value) × KV heads × head dim × bytes per element: 36 × 2 × 8 × 128 × 2 = 147456.
    10	
    11	What the forward does:
    12	
    13	- Prefill Π once. Keep that KV. Every later forward clones it and discards the clone, so Π is not rewritten.
    14	- A block is one lookup. D and M blocks are 2 tokens. S blocks are 4 tokens (carry-in, digit, digit, carry-out).
    15	- position ids inside a block restart at |Π|, the same positions as the pinned single tick. The cache itself appends in order. The restarted positions are RoPE positions, not the write index.
    16	- The mask is 4-dimensional, shape (1, 1, query tokens, |Π| + query tokens), boolean, True where attention is allowed. Each query may attend to every Π key and, causally, to the keys of its own block. It may not attend to any other block. One block reproduces the pinned tick.
    17	- The argmax is read at the last token of each block. The logits of the other tokens can be dropped.
    18	
    19	Π lengths from the algebra record: D 400 tokens, M 36 tokens, S 1200 tokens. The deployed chunk is 4096 query tokens.
    20	
    21	Resident KV for one deployed forward, Π plus one chunk:
    22	- D: 400 + 4096 = 4496 tokens, 662962176 bytes.
    23	- M: 36 + 4096 = 4132 tokens, 609288192 bytes.
    24	- S: 1200 + 4096 = 5296 tokens, 780926976 bytes.
    25	
    26	A single forward of the widest Hillis–Steele round at 4096 digits holds 4095 lookups × 2 tokens = 8190 query tokens, plus the M prefix of 36 tokens: 8226 tokens, 1212973056 bytes. The run does not do that. It cuts the round into chunks of the deployed width, and the resident set is the chunk line above. The browser can do the same.
3:The packed path prefills Π once, then one forward holds many blocks. Each block's positions restart at |Π|,
4:and a block-diagonal mask lets it see only Π and itself. The batched path repeats Π in every row. Both are

   420	### Corrupted 4096-digit sum
   421	
   422	`scan-counted2-h100/demo.json`, seed 20261028, host NVIDIA H100 80GB HBM3; unknown; GenuineIntel 6 143 unknown; avx512f=True, n_digits 4096, instance 0. Ledger gpu NVIDIA H100 80GB HBM3, cpu GenuineIntel | 6 | 143 | unknown | avx512f=True. Planted row KK->G (true output K).
   423	Model Hillis–Steele sum equals the algebra schedule: True. Model Blelloch sum equals the algebra schedule: True. The file's own flags were True and True. Both differ from the bigint sum: True. The two schedules differ from each other: True.
   424	- BL: n 12286, n_match 12286, min_margin 2.25.
   425	- D: n 4096, n_match 4096, min_margin 1.5.
   426	- HS: n 45057, n_match 45057, min_margin 2.25.
   427	- S_BL: n 4096, n_match 4096, min_margin 9.375.
   428	- S_HS: n 4096, n_match 4096, min_margin 9.375.
   429	Lookups matched the corrupted prefix: True. Recomputed propagation equals the stored where-record: True.
   430	- seq: n_used 595, n_cin_diff 668, every wrong column is strictly after a use True, every use poisons the next column True, wrong columns beyond that next column 73, cin_diff equals the use columns False, final carry differs False.
   431	- hs: n_used 1726, n_cin_diff 1734, every wrong column is strictly after a use True, every use poisons the next column True, wrong columns beyond that next column 8, cin_diff equals the use columns False, final carry differs False.
   432	- bl: n_used 1305, n_cin_diff 606, every wrong column is strictly after a use True, every use poisons the next column False, wrong columns beyond that next column 65, cin_diff equals the use columns False, final carry differs False.
   433	Wrong sum digits are the columns whose carry-in differs, plus the leading digit when the final carry differs. They are not the columns where the corrupted row was read. A use writes a prefix, and the following columns stay wrong through later P blocks until a K or G is absorbed. The counts above are that closure, recomputed from the saved operands.
   434	
   435	## Theory
   436	
   437	The carrier is {K, P, G}. D sends a digit pair (a, b) to K when a+b ≤ 8, P when a+b = 9, and G when a+b ≥ 10. M is the binary operation x ⊕ y = y when y is not P, and x when y is P. P is a two-sided identity: x ⊕ P = x and P ⊕ x = x. S is the ordinary sum digit of (carry-in, a, b).
   438	
   439	Associativity of M. Let x, y, z be any of the three symbols. If z is not P, then (x ⊕ y) ⊕ z = z and x ⊕ (y ⊕ z) = x ⊕ z = z. If z is P, then (x ⊕ y) ⊕ z = x ⊕ y and x ⊕ (y ⊕ z) = x ⊕ y. The two sides agree in every case, so every parenthesization of a word agrees. Light's test on the true table finds 0 failing triples.
   440	
   441	Carry. With the carry into a block equal to 0, the carry out is 1 exactly when the block is G. One column: the column is G exactly when a+b ≥ 10, which is exactly when the carry out is 1 and the carry in was 0. Several columns: write the word as a low block followed by a high block. If the high block is K or G, the product is the high block, so the carry out is the high block's own carry out, which does not depend on the carry into the low block. That is what K and G mean. If the high block is P, the product is the low block, so the carry out equals the low block's carry out, which is the carry that P lets through. By induction on the word, the carry out of a prefix is 1 exactly when the prefix is G, provided the carry into the whole sum is 0.
   442	
   443	Theorem P², sufficiency. Suppose every row of D, M and S is certified on the path that the scan actually runs, and M is the associative operation above. D assigns each column its true block. Any evaluation tree of M, including Hillis–Steele and Blelloch, computes the same prefixes, because every parenthesization agrees. The carry into each column is then the true carry. S, given that carry, the two digits, and the carry out that those three determine, returns the true sum digit. The final carry is the leading digit. So every bracketing and every schedule returns the exact sum.
   444	
   445	Necessity, corrected. Each of the 9 rows is the unique lookup in a 2-column input (necessity_all_rows_used True). A wrong output changes some sum exactly when it leaves the carry class of the true output. G is its own class; K and P both emit carry 0. There are 5 same-class corruptions (KK->P, KP->P, PK->P, PP->K, GK->P) and 13 carry-crossing corruptions (KK->G, KP->G, KG->K, KG->P, PK->G, PP->G, PG->K, PG->P, GK->G, GP->K, GP->P, GG->K, GG->P). necessity_carry_crossing_changes_sum True. necessity_same_carry_class_preserves_sum True. A K↔P error does not change any sum. Light's test is a different predicate: 4 corruptions preserve associativity (KP->G, KG->K, GK->G, GP->K), and of those the ones that still change a sum are the carry-crossing ones (KP->G, KG->K, GK->G, GP->K). An uncertified wrong row makes some input wrong when the wrong symbol crosses the carry class. It does not, when the wrong symbol stays in the class.

 succeeded in 0ms:
     1	"""Packed and batched lookups on a fresh merge of Savant r3.
     2	
     3	The packed path prefills Π once, then one forward holds many blocks. Each block's positions restart at |Π|,
     4	and a block-diagonal mask lets it see only Π and itself. The batched path repeats Π in every row. Both are
     5	bf16 SDPA, the same runtime as the pinned tick.
     6	
     7	Torch is imported inside the functions that need it. Nothing here launches a job.
     8	"""
     9	
    10	from __future__ import annotations

    18	MODEL_NAME = "Qwen/Qwen3-4B"
    19	ADAPTER = "/arvol/arith-s1r2/adapter_step500"
    20	MERGE_DIR = "/tmp/pb_scan_merged"
    21	# Query tokens per forward. Logits are (Q, vocab) bf16; Q = 4096 is about 1.2 GiB.
    22	QUERY_TOKENS = 4096
    23	COSINE_MAX = 0.3
    24	# How many cosine-ordered triples a job will certify before it stops looking.
    25	# The first 200 were almost all the same K token, so the walk has to go much further.
    26	SEARCH_LIMIT = 8000

    30	    return max(1, QUERY_TOKENS // block_len)

    52	def load_merged(model_name=MODEL_NAME, adapter=ADAPTER):
    53	    """Merge the adapter into a new directory and load it. A warm container must not reuse a previous merge."""
    54	    import torch
    55	    from pb_eval import merge_adapter
    56	    from transformers import AutoModelForCausalLM
    57	    if os.path.exists(MERGE_DIR):
    58	        shutil.rmtree(MERGE_DIR)
    59	    merge_adapter(model_name, adapter, MERGE_DIR)
    60	    model = AutoModelForCausalLM.from_pretrained(
    61	        MERGE_DIR, dtype=torch.bfloat16, attn_implementation="sdpa").cuda().eval()
    62	    return model

    92	def _rank_triples(table, ids, limit):
    93	    """Triples with pairwise cosine <= 0.3, ordered by (max pairwise cosine, sorted token ids)."""
    94	    n = len(ids)
    95	    keys = []
    96	    for i in range(n):
    97	        row_i = table[i]
    98	        for j in range(i + 1, n):
    99	            cij = row_i[j]
   100	            row_j = table[j]
   101	            for k in range(j + 1, n):
   102	                m = cij
   103	                if row_i[k] > m:
   104	                    m = row_i[k]
   105	                if row_j[k] > m:
   106	                    m = row_j[k]
   107	                if m > COSINE_MAX:
   108	                    continue
   109	                trip = tuple(sorted((int(ids[i]), int(ids[j]), int(ids[k]))))
   110	                keys.append((m, trip))
   111	    keys.sort()
   112	    return keys[:limit]
   113	
   114	
   115	def _pool_ids(names, id_of, banned):
   116	    out = []
   117	    seen = set()
   118	    for name in names:
   119	        i = int(id_of[name])
   120	        if i in banned or i in seen:
   121	            continue
   122	        seen.add(i)
   123	        out.append(i)
   124	    return out
   125	
   126	
   127	def _ranked_from_model(model, tok, limit):
   128	    """Cosine order inside the first Moore pool that has a legal triple. Test pool, then train pool."""
   129	    import torch
   130	    import pb_pin
   131	    weight = model.get_input_embeddings().weight.detach()
   132	    dig = digit_token_ids(tok)
   133	    nl = newline_id(tok)
   134	    banned = set(dig) | {nl}
   135	    pool = pb_pin.name_pool(tok)
   136	    for split in ("test", "train"):
   137	        ids = _pool_ids(pool[split], pool["id_of"], banned)
   138	        if len(ids) < 3:
   139	            continue
   140	        idx = torch.tensor(ids, device=weight.device)
   141	        x = weight.index_select(0, idx).float()
   142	        x = x / x.norm(dim=-1, keepdim=True).clamp(min=1e-8)
   143	        table = (x @ x.T).cpu().tolist()
   144	        del x
   145	        ranked = _rank_triples(table, ids, limit)
   146	        if ranked:
   147	            return ranked, split, weight, dig, nl
   148	    raise RuntimeError("no K/P/G triple with pairwise cosine <= 0.3 in the Moore name pools")
   149	
   150	
   151	def _symbol_record(model, tok, weight, trip, pool, sort_key, dig, nl):
   225	def _pi(rows, nl):
   226	    pi = []
   227	    for row in rows:
   228	        pi.extend(row)
   229	        pi.append(nl)
   230	    return pi
   231	
   232	
   233	def build_tables(symbols):
   234	    """D and M are Moore rows (left, right, out, newline). S reuses the adder's five-token rows.
   235	
   236	    The deployed S query is the one forward [c, a, b, c'] reading the sum digit, which is the pinned
   237	    forward 2. c' is the carry the scan already computed, so S is one packed round.
   238	    """
   239	    dig = symbols["digit_ids"]
   240	    sym = symbols["id"]
   241	    nl = symbols["nl"]
   242	    d_rows, d_expect = [], {}
   243	    for a, b, out in alg.digit_rows():
   244	        row = [dig[a], dig[b], sym[out]]
   245	        d_rows.append(row)
   246	        d_expect[(dig[a], dig[b])] = sym[out]
   247	    m_rows, m_expect = [], {}
   248	    for low, high, out in alg.monoid_rows():
   249	        row = [sym[low], sym[high], sym[out]]
   250	        m_rows.append(row)
   251	        m_expect[(sym[low], sym[high])] = sym[out]
   252	    s_rows, s_expect = [], {}
   253	    for c, a, b, cout, s in alg.adder_rows():
   254	        row = [dig[c], dig[a], dig[b], dig[cout], dig[s]]
   255	        s_rows.append(row)
   256	        s_expect[(dig[c], dig[a], dig[b], dig[cout])] = dig[s]
   257	    def pack(rows, expect, block_len):
   258	        return {"rows": rows, "expect": expect, "pi": _pi(rows, nl), "block_len": block_len,
   259	                "queries": [row[:block_len] for row in rows]}
   260	    # D and M queries are the first two tokens; S query is the first four (c a b c'), not the sum digit.
   261	    return {
   262	        "D": pack(d_rows, d_expect, 2),
   263	        "M": pack(m_rows, m_expect, 2),
   264	        "S": pack(s_rows, s_expect, 4),
   265	        "symbols": symbols,
   266	    }
   267	
   268	
   434	def corrupt_m(tables, low, high, wrong):
   435	    """A copy of M whose one row (low, high) writes `wrong` instead of the true product."""
   436	    sym = tables["symbols"]["id"]
   437	    nl = tables["symbols"]["nl"]
   438	    src = tables["M"]
   439	    rows = [list(r) for r in src["rows"]]
   440	    key = (sym[low], sym[high])
   441	    found = False
   442	    for row in rows:
   443	        if (row[0], row[1]) == key:
   444	            row[2] = sym[wrong]
   445	            found = True
   446	    if not found:
   447	        raise RuntimeError("row missing")
   448	    expect = dict(src["expect"])
   449	    expect[key] = sym[wrong]
   450	    return {"rows": rows, "expect": expect, "pi": _pi(rows, nl), "block_len": 2,
   451	            "queries": [row[:2] for row in rows]}
   452	
   453	
   454	# ---------------------------------------------------------------- forwards
   455	
   456	
   457	def allow_mask(P, n_blocks, block_len, device):
   458	    """Bool mask, True = may attend, shape (1, 1, Q, P+Q). Matches alg.attention_allows."""
   459	    import torch
   460	    Q = n_blocks * block_len
   461	    K = P + Q
   462	    allow = torch.zeros((1, 1, Q, K), dtype=torch.bool, device=device)
   463	    if Q == 0:
   464	        return allow
   465	    allow[:, :, :, :P] = True
   466	    q = torch.arange(Q, device=device)
   467	    k = torch.arange(Q, device=device)
   468	    bq = torch.div(q, block_len, rounding_mode="floor")
   469	    bk = torch.div(k, block_len, rounding_mode="floor")
   470	    off_q = q - bq * block_len
   471	    off_k = k - bk * block_len
   472	    local = (bq[:, None] == bk[None, :]) & (off_k[None, :] <= off_q[:, None])
   473	    allow[0, 0, :, P:] = local
   474	    if Q * K <= 2_000_000:
   475	        got = int(allow.sum().item())
   476	        want = alg.allow_count(P, n_blocks, block_len)
   477	        if got != want:
   478	            raise RuntimeError(f"mask count {got} != {want} for P={P} B={n_blocks} block={block_len}")
   479	    return allow
   480	
   481	
   482	def prefill(model, pi_ids):
   483	    import torch
   484	    import pb_pin
   485	    device = _device(model)
   486	    ids = torch.tensor([pi_ids], device=device)
   487	    pos = torch.arange(len(pi_ids), device=device)[None]
   488	    with torch.inference_mode():
   489	        past = model(input_ids=ids, position_ids=pos, use_cache=True).past_key_values
   490	    if past.get_seq_length() != len(pi_ids):
   491	        raise RuntimeError(f"prefill length {past.get_seq_length()} != {len(pi_ids)}")
   492	    return past
   493	
   494	
   495	def _summarize(ends):
   496	    """ends: (B, V) bf16. Returns list of (argmax, margin) using float32 top-2, in chunks."""
   497	    import torch
   498	    out = []
   499	    for i in range(0, ends.shape[0], 256):
   500	        chunk = ends[i:i + 256].float()
   501	        vals, idx = torch.topk(chunk, 2, dim=-1)
   502	        for j in range(chunk.shape[0]):
   503	            out.append((int(idx[j, 0]), float(vals[j, 0] - vals[j, 1])))
   504	        del chunk
   505	    return out
   506	
   507	
   508	def _block_ends(logits, n_blocks, block_len):
   509	    import torch
   510	    idx = torch.arange(n_blocks, device=logits.device) * block_len + (block_len - 1)
   511	    return logits.index_select(0, idx)
   512	
   513	
   514	def packed_lookup(model, past, P, blocks):
   515	    """One forward. `blocks` share `past` (cloned here, so Π is not written). Logits at each block's last token."""
   516	    import torch
   517	    import pb_pin
   518	    if not blocks:
   519	        return [], 0.0
   520	    block_len = len(blocks[0])
   521	    if any(len(b) != block_len for b in blocks):
   522	        raise RuntimeError("mixed block lengths")
   523	    device = _device(model)
   524	    ids = [t for b in blocks for t in b]
   525	    pos = []
   526	    for _ in blocks:
   527	        pos.extend(range(P, P + block_len))
   528	    mask = allow_mask(P, len(blocks), block_len, device)
   529	    cache = pb_pin._clone(past)
   530	    before = past.get_seq_length()
   531	    input_ids = torch.tensor([ids], device=device)
   532	    position_ids = torch.tensor([pos], device=device)
   533	    _sync()
   534	    t0 = time.perf_counter()
   535	    with torch.inference_mode():
   536	        out = model(input_ids=input_ids, position_ids=position_ids, attention_mask=mask,
   537	                    past_key_values=cache, use_cache=True)
   538	    _sync()
   539	    seconds = time.perf_counter() - t0
   540	    if past.get_seq_length() != before:
   541	        raise RuntimeError("packed forward wrote the shared Π cache")
   542	    ends = _block_ends(out.logits[0], len(blocks), block_len)
   543	    summary = _summarize(ends)
   544	    del out, cache, ends
   545	    return summary, seconds
   546	
   547	
   548	def batched_lookup(model, pi, blocks):
   549	    """Π repeated in every row. Reference path, not the deployed one."""
   550	    import torch
   551	    if not blocks:
   552	        return [], 0.0
   553	    block_len = len(blocks[0])
   554	    P = len(pi)
   555	    device = _device(model)
   556	    rows = [pi + list(b) for b in blocks]
   557	    pos = [list(range(P + block_len)) for _ in blocks]
   558	    input_ids = torch.tensor(rows, device=device)
   559	    position_ids = torch.tensor(pos, device=device)
   560	    _sync()
   561	    t0 = time.perf_counter()
   562	    with torch.inference_mode():
   563	        out = model(input_ids=input_ids, position_ids=position_ids, use_cache=False)
   564	    _sync()
   565	    seconds = time.perf_counter() - t0
   566	    summary = _summarize(out.logits[:, -1, :])
   567	    del out
   568	    return summary, seconds
   569	
   570	
   571	def single_lookup(model, past, P, block):
   572	    """The pinned tick: one block, the model's own causal mask, positions restarted. Returns the logit vector."""
   573	    import torch
   574	    import pb_pin
   575	    device = _device(model)
   576	    ids = torch.tensor([list(block)], device=device)
   577	    pos = torch.tensor([list(range(P, P + len(block)))], device=device)
   578	    cache = pb_pin._clone(past)
   579	    with torch.inference_mode():
   580	        out = model(input_ids=ids, position_ids=pos, cache_position=pos[0], past_key_values=cache, use_cache=True)
   581	    logit = out.logits[0, -1].clone()
   582	    del out, cache
   583	    return logit
   584	
   585	
   586	def chunked_packed(model, past, P, blocks, chunk):
   587	    summary = []
   588	    pieces = []
   589	    for i in range(0, len(blocks), chunk):
   590	        part, sec = packed_lookup(model, past, P, blocks[i:i + chunk])
   591	        summary.extend(part)
   592	        pieces.append(sec)
   593	    return summary, pieces
   594	
   595	
   596	def _add_seconds(stats, pieces):
   597	    stats["seconds"] += sum(pieces)
   598	    stats.setdefault("chunk_seconds", []).extend(pieces)
   599	
   600	
   601	def _logit_delta(a, b):
   602	    import torch
   603	    return bool(torch.equal(a, b)), float((a.float() - b.float()).abs().max())
   604	
   605	
   606	# ---------------------------------------------------------------- certification and invariance
   607	
   608	
   609	def _note(stats, margin, match, detail):
   610	    stats["n"] += 1
   611	    stats["n_match"] += int(match)
   612	    add_margin(stats, margin)
   613	    if not match and len(stats["bad"]) < 12:
   614	        stats["bad"].append(detail)
   615	
   616	
   617	def packed_end_logits(model, past, P, blocks):
   618	    """End-of-block logit vectors, (B, V) bf16, and the forward seconds. Caller owns the tensor."""
   619	    import torch
   620	    import pb_pin
   621	    block_len = len(blocks[0])
   622	    device = _device(model)
   623	    ids = [t for b in blocks for t in b]
   624	    pos = []
   625	    for _ in blocks:
   626	        pos.extend(range(P, P + block_len))
   627	    mask = allow_mask(P, len(blocks), block_len, device)
   628	    cache = pb_pin._clone(past)
   629	    before = past.get_seq_length()
   630	    _sync()
   631	    t0 = time.perf_counter()
   632	    with torch.inference_mode():
   633	        out = model(input_ids=torch.tensor([ids], device=device),
   634	                    position_ids=torch.tensor([pos], device=device),
   635	                    attention_mask=mask, past_key_values=cache, use_cache=True)
   636	    _sync()
   637	    seconds = time.perf_counter() - t0
   638	    if past.get_seq_length() != before:
   639	        raise RuntimeError("packed forward wrote the shared Π cache")
   640	    ends = _block_ends(out.logits[0], len(blocks), block_len).clone()
   641	    del out, cache
   642	    return ends, seconds
   643	
   644	
   645	def _absorb(report, equal, delta, margin, argmax_ok):
   681	def measure_invariance(model, tables, widths=None, row_limit=None, include_chunk=True):
   682	    """Largest |Δlogit| across slot, width B, and packed-versus-batched, against the pinned single tick.
   683	
   684	    Certifies every touched slot: argmax equals the row and the margin is positive. `row_limit` keeps a smoke
   685	    run small; None means every row.
   686	    """
   687	    report = {"tables": {}, "n_compared": 0, "n_equal": 0, "max_abs_delta": 0.0, "min_margin": None,
   688	              "n_argmax_mismatch": 0, "widths": None}
   689	    for name in ("D", "M", "S"):
   690	        table = tables[name]
   691	        past = prefill(model, table["pi"])
   692	        P = len(table["pi"])
   693	        queries = table["queries"]
   694	        bl = table["block_len"]
   695	        chunk = chunk_for(bl)
   696	        use = queries if row_limit is None else queries[:row_limit]
   697	        targets = [q_row[bl] for q_row in (table["rows"] if row_limit is None else table["rows"][:row_limit])]
   698	        # targets: D/M row[2] and the query is row[:2], so row[bl] is the output. S row[4] with bl=4.
   699	        refs = [single_lookup(model, past, P, q) for q in use]
   700	        widths_here = list(widths or (1, 8, 32))
   701	        if include_chunk and chunk not in widths_here:
   702	            widths_here.append(chunk)
   703	        table_rec = {"rows": len(use), "P": P, "chunk": chunk, "widths": widths_here,
   704	                     "b1_argmax_bad": 0, "cert_fail": 0}
   705	        for b in widths_here:
   706	            # every slot of an all-equal forward, for every row, then one mixed forward
   707	            for i, (q, target) in enumerate(zip(use, targets)):
   708	                ends, _ = packed_end_logits(model, past, P, [q] * b)
   709	                fails = _absorb_ends(report, ends, refs[i], target)
   710	                table_rec["cert_fail"] += fails
   711	                if b == 1:
   712	                    table_rec["b1_argmax_bad"] += fails
   713	                del ends
   714	            mixed_blocks = [use[i % len(use)] for i in range(b)]
   715	            ends, _ = packed_end_logits(model, past, P, mixed_blocks)
   716	            for s in range(0, b, 128):
   717	                part = ends[s:s + 128]
   718	                for j in range(part.shape[0]):
   719	                    i = (s + j) % len(use)
   720	                    arg, margin = _top(part[j])
   721	                    equal, delta = _logit_delta(part[j], refs[i])
   722	                    ok = arg == targets[i] and margin > 0
   723	                    _absorb(report, equal, delta, margin, ok)
   724	                    table_rec["cert_fail"] += int(not ok)
   725	            del ends
   726	        # batched reference, Π repeated, small width so the repeated prefix stays cheap
   727	        batch_w = min(4, len(use))
   728	        for start in range(0, len(use), batch_w):
   729	            group = use[start:start + batch_w]
   730	            import torch
   731	            device = _device(model)
   732	            rows = [table["pi"] + list(q) for q in group]
   733	            pos = [list(range(P + bl)) for _ in group]
   734	            with torch.inference_mode():
   735	                out = model(input_ids=torch.tensor(rows, device=device),
   736	                            position_ids=torch.tensor(pos, device=device), use_cache=False)
   737	            for j, q in enumerate(group):
   738	                arg, margin = _top(out.logits[j, -1])
   739	                equal, delta = _logit_delta(out.logits[j, -1], refs[start + j])
   740	                ok = arg == targets[start + j] and margin > 0
   741	                _absorb(report, equal, delta, margin, ok)
   742	                table_rec["cert_fail"] += int(not ok)
   743	            del out
   744	        table_rec["pass"] = table_rec["cert_fail"] == 0 and table_rec["b1_argmax_bad"] == 0
   745	        report["tables"][name] = table_rec
   746	        del refs, past
   747	        torch_empty()
   748	    report["include_chunk"] = include_chunk
   749	    report["bitwise"] = report["n_compared"] > 0 and report["n_equal"] == report["n_compared"] and report["max_abs_delta"] == 0.0
   750	    bound = 2.0 * report["max_abs_delta"]
   751	    report["margin_bound"] = bound
   752	    report["margin_ok"] = report["min_margin"] is not None and report["min_margin"] > bound
   753	    if report["bitwise"]:
   754	        report["decision"] = "bitwise"
   755	        report["empirical_bound"] = False
   756	        report["decision_ok"] = True
   757	    else:
   758	        report["decision"] = "margin"
   759	        report["empirical_bound"] = True
   760	        report["decision_ok"] = bool(report["margin_ok"]) and report["n_argmax_mismatch"] == 0
   761	    return report
   762	
   772	def _consume(summary, expect, stats):
   773	    """summary is a list of (argmax, margin) aligned with (low, high) keys in `keys`."""
   774	    out_ids = []
   775	    for (arg, margin), key in zip(summary, expect):
   776	        target = key
   777	        match = target is not None and arg == target and margin > 0
   778	        if target is None:
   779	            stats["n_missing"] += 1
   780	        _note(stats, margin, match, {"arg": arg, "target": target, "margin": margin})
   781	        out_ids.append(arg)
   782	    return out_ids
   783	
   784	
   785	def lookup_blocks(model, past, P, blocks, targets, stats, chunk):
   786	    summary, pieces = chunked_packed(model, past, P, blocks, chunk)
   787	    _add_seconds(stats, pieces)
   788	    return _consume(summary, targets, stats)
   789	
   790	
   791	def run_schedule(model, past, values, rounds, original, identity_id, expect, stats, chunk):
   792	    """`expect` maps (low_id, high_id) to the output id Π says. The array follows the model's argmax."""
   793	    a = list(values)
   794	    original = list(original)
   795	    P = past.get_seq_length()
   796	    for rnd in rounds:
   797	        actions = alg.plan_round(a, original, rnd)
   798	        lookups = [act for act in actions if act[0] == "lookup"]
   799	        summary = []
   800	        if lookups:
   801	            blocks = [[low, high] for _, _, low, high in lookups]
   802	            summary, pieces = chunked_packed(model, past, P, blocks, chunk)
   803	            _add_seconds(stats, pieces)
   804	        new = list(a)
   805	        li = 0
   806	        for act in actions:
   807	            if act[0] == "lookup":
   808	                _, dest, low, high = act
   809	                arg, margin = summary[li]
   810	                li += 1
   811	                target = expect.get((low, high))
   812	                match = target is not None and arg == target and margin > 0
   813	                if target is None:
   814	                    stats["n_missing"] += 1
   815	                if not match and len(stats["bad"]) < 8:
   816	                    stats["bad"].append({"low": low, "high": high, "arg": arg, "target": target, "margin": margin})
   817	                _note_silent(stats, margin, match)
   818	                new[dest] = arg
   819	            elif act[0] == "value":
   820	                _, dest, value = act
   821	                new[dest] = value
   822	            elif act[0] == "identity":
   823	                new[act[1]] = identity_id
   824	        a = new
   825	    return a
   826	
   827	
   828	def _note_silent(stats, margin, match):
   829	    stats["n"] += 1
   830	    stats["n_match"] += int(match)
   831	    add_margin(stats, margin)
   832	
   833	
   834	def prefix_ids_to_symbols(prefix, id_to_name):
   835	    return [id_to_name[i] for i in prefix]
   836	
   837	
   838	def carries_from_prefixes(prefix_ids, g_id, zero_id, one_id):
   839	    cin_ids = []
   840	    cout_ids = []
   841	    cin = zero_id
   842	    for p in prefix_ids:
   843	        cin_ids.append(cin)
   844	        cout = one_id if p == g_id else zero_id
   845	        cout_ids.append(cout)
   846	        cin = cout
   847	    final = 1 if cin == one_id else 0
   848	    return cin_ids, cout_ids, final
   849	
   850	
   851	def sum_from_digit_ids(digit_ids, final_carry, id_to_digit):
   852	    chars = []
   853	    for i in digit_ids:
   854	        if i not in id_to_digit:
   855	            return None
   856	        chars.append(str(id_to_digit[i]))
   857	    if final_carry:
   858	        chars.append("1")
   859	    text = "".join(reversed(chars)).lstrip("0")
   860	    return text or "0"
   861	
   862	
   863	def run_addition(model, tables, a, b, margin_floor=None, m_table=None):
   864	    """One addition. D once, then Hillis–Steele and Blelloch, then S for each schedule's carries.
   865	
   866	    `m_table` replaces M (a corrupted prefix). Lookups are scored against that table, so a corrupted row
   867	    matches when the model copies the corrupted prefix.
   868	    """
   869	    sym = tables["symbols"]
   870	    dig = sym["digit_ids"]
   871	    id_to_digit = {i: d for d, i in enumerate(dig)}
   872	    g_id = sym["id"]["G"]
   873	    pairs = alg.columns(a, b)
   874	    n = len(pairs)
   875	    stats = {"D": new_stats(), "HS": new_stats(), "BL": new_stats(), "S_HS": new_stats(), "S_BL": new_stats()}
   876	
   877	    d_table = tables["D"]
   878	    past_d = prefill(model, d_table["pi"])
   879	    d_blocks = [[dig[x], dig[y]] for x, y in pairs]
   880	    d_targets = [d_table["expect"].get(tuple(b)) for b in d_blocks]
   881	    d_ids = lookup_blocks(model, past_d, len(d_table["pi"]), d_blocks, d_targets, stats["D"], chunk_for(2))
   882	    del past_d
   883	
   884	    def one_scan(kind, rounds, values):
   885	        m = tables["M"] if m_table is None else m_table
   886	        past = prefill(model, m["pi"])
   887	        padded_n = len(values)
   888	        original = list(values)
   889	        got = run_schedule(model, past, values, rounds, original, sym["id"]["P"], m["expect"], stats[kind], chunk_for(2))
   890	        del past
   891	        return got[:n] if padded_n != n else got
   892	
   893	    hs_rounds = alg.hillis_schedule(n)
   894	    hs_ids = one_scan("HS", hs_rounds, list(d_ids))
   895	    pad, bl_rounds = alg.blelloch_schedule(n)
   896	    bl_values = list(d_ids) + [sym["id"]["P"]] * (pad - n)
   897	    bl_ids = one_scan("BL", bl_rounds, bl_values)
   898	
   899	    def s_round(prefix_ids, label):
   900	        # The carry into column 0 is 0. The carry into a later column is the scan prefix's carry.
   901	        # The cout token in the S query is the adder row's own cout for that (cin, a, b), so the query
   902	        # addresses a real row. On a correct monoid this cout equals the prefix's carry.
   903	        cin_bits = []
   904	        cin = 0
   905	        scan_cout = []
   906	        for p in prefix_ids:
   907	            cin_bits.append(cin)
   908	            bit = 1 if p == g_id else 0
   909	            scan_cout.append(bit)
   910	            cin = bit
   911	        final = cin
   912	        s = tables["S"]
   913	        past = prefill(model, s["pi"])
   914	        blocks = []
   915	        targets = []
   916	        adder_cout = []
   917	        for i, (x, y) in enumerate(pairs):
   918	            cout = 1 if cin_bits[i] + x + y >= 10 else 0
   919	            adder_cout.append(cout)
   920	            block = [dig[cin_bits[i]], dig[x], dig[y], dig[cout]]
   921	            blocks.append(block)
   922	            targets.append(s["expect"].get(tuple(block)))
   923	        outs = lookup_blocks(model, past, len(s["pi"]), blocks, targets, stats[label], chunk_for(4))
   924	        del past
   925	        text = sum_from_digit_ids(outs, final, id_to_digit)
   926	        return text, final, scan_cout == adder_cout
   927	
   928	    hs_sum, hs_final, hs_carry_ok = s_round(hs_ids, "S_HS")
   929	    bl_sum, bl_final, bl_carry_ok = s_round(bl_ids, "S_BL")
   930	    truth = alg.bigint_sum(a, b)
   931	    margin_holds = True
   932	    if margin_floor is not None:
   933	        for st in stats.values():
   934	            if st["n"] and (st["min_margin"] is None or st["min_margin"] <= margin_floor):
   935	                margin_holds = False
   936	    return {
   937	        "n_digits": n,
   938	        "hs_sum": hs_sum,
   939	        "bl_sum": bl_sum,
   940	        "bigint": truth,
   941	        "hs_exact": hs_sum == truth,
   942	        "bl_exact": bl_sum == truth,
   943	        "hs_carry_matches_adder": hs_carry_ok,
   944	        "bl_carry_matches_adder": bl_carry_ok,
   945	        "schedules_agree": hs_sum == bl_sum and hs_ids == bl_ids,
   946	        "prefixes_agree": hs_ids == bl_ids,
   947	        "hs_rounds": len(alg.model_rounds(hs_rounds)) + 2,
   948	        "bl_rounds": len(alg.model_rounds(bl_rounds)) + 2,
   949	        "stats": stats,
   950	        "margin_holds": margin_holds,
   951	        "model_seconds": sum(st["seconds"] for st in stats.values()),
   952	    }
   953	
   954	
   955	def light_on_model(model, corrupted, sym_ids):
   956	    """27 triples as model lookups against one corrupted M. Two waves: the inner products, then the outer ones."""
   957	    past = prefill(model, corrupted["pi"])
   958	    P = len(corrupted["pi"])
   959	    names = ("K", "P", "G")
   960	    triples = [(x, y, z) for x in names for y in names for z in names]
   961	    id_of = sym_ids
   962	    wave1 = []
   963	    for x, y, z in triples:
   964	        wave1.append([id_of[x], id_of[y]])
   965	        wave1.append([id_of[y], id_of[z]])
   966	    got1, pieces1 = chunked_packed(model, past, P, wave1, chunk_for(2))
   967	    wave2 = []
   968	    for t, (x, y, z) in enumerate(triples):
   969	        wave2.append([got1[2 * t][0], id_of[z]])
   970	        wave2.append([id_of[x], got1[2 * t + 1][0]])
   971	    got2, pieces2 = chunked_packed(model, past, P, wave2, chunk_for(2))
   972	    fails = []
   973	    n_match = 0
   974	    n = 0
   975	    min_margin = None
   976	    for t, (x, y, z) in enumerate(triples):
   977	        left_id = got2[2 * t][0]
   978	        right_id = got2[2 * t + 1][0]
   979	        quads = (
   980	            (got1[2 * t], (id_of[x], id_of[y])),
   981	            (got1[2 * t + 1], (id_of[y], id_of[z])),
   982	            (got2[2 * t], (got1[2 * t][0], id_of[z])),
   983	            (got2[2 * t + 1], (id_of[x], got1[2 * t + 1][0])),
   984	        )
   985	        for (arg, margin), (low, high) in quads:
   986	            n += 1
   987	            min_margin = margin if min_margin is None else min(min_margin, margin)
   988	            target = corrupted["expect"].get((low, high))
   989	            n_match += int(target is not None and arg == target and margin > 0)
   990	        if left_id != right_id:
   991	            fails.append(x + y + z)
   992	    del past
   993	    return {"fails": fails, "n_fail": len(fails), "n_lookups": n, "n_match_pi": n_match,
   994	            "min_margin": min_margin, "seconds": sum(pieces1) + sum(pieces2),
   995	            "chunk_seconds": pieces1 + pieces2}
   996	
   997

   123	def run_faults(model, tables):

   125	    for c in alg.corruptions():

   146	def margin_floor_of(cert):

   152	def b1_bad(cert):
   153	    return any(table.get("b1_argmax_bad", 0) for table in cert.get("tables", {}).values())

   156	def smoke(out, model, tables, host, arch, symbols):
   157	    rec = with_pi(common("smoke", host, arch, symbols), tables)
   158	    cert = fwd.measure_invariance(model, tables, widths=(1, 8), row_limit=4, include_chunk=False)
   159	    rec["invariance"] = cert
   160	    rec["b1_ok"] = not b1_bad(cert)
   161	    chunks = {name: time_chunk(model, tables[name]) for name in ("D", "M", "S")}
   162	    rec["chunks"] = chunks
   163	    rec["extrapolation_100000"] = extrapolate([chunks[n]["seconds"] for n in ("D", "M", "S")])
   164	    a, b = alg.draw_instances(SEED, 32, 1)[0]
   165	    rec["addition_32"] = addition_record(model, tables, a, b, None)
   166	    demo = alg.demo_choice(SEED, 4096, 16)
   167	    corrupted = fwd.corrupt_m(tables, demo["low"], demo["high"], demo["wrong"])
   168	    light = fwd.light_on_model(model, corrupted, symbols["id"])
   169	    rec["one_fault"] = {"name": f"{demo['low']}{demo['high']}->{demo['wrong']}", "algebra_fails": demo["fails"],
   170	                        "fails": light["fails"], "matches_algebra": light["fails"] == demo["fails"],
   171	                        "n_lookups": light["n_lookups"], "n_match_pi": light["n_match_pi"],
   172	                        "min_margin": light["min_margin"], "seconds": light["seconds"]}
   173	    atomic_json(out / "smoke.json", rec)
   174	    print(f"smoke b1_ok={rec['b1_ok']} exact32={rec['addition_32']['hs_exact']} "
   175	          f"extrap={rec['extrapolation_100000']['extrapolated_hs_seconds']:.2f}s", flush=True)
   176	    if not rec["b1_ok"]:
   177	        sys.exit(2)

   180	def counted(out, model, tables, host, arch, symbols):
   260	    rec["tables"] = [fwd.probe_table(model, tok, tables[name], name) for name in ("D", "M", "S")]
   261	    atomic_json(out / "probe.json", rec)
   262	    n_bad = sum(len(t["bad"]) for t in rec["tables"])
   263	    print(f"probe bad={n_bad}", flush=True)
   264	    for t in rec["tables"]:
   265	        print(f"  {t['table']} ok={t['n_ok']}/{t['n']} min_margin={t['min_margin']}", flush=True)
   266	        for row in t["bad"][:8]:
   267	            print(f"    i={row['i']} query={row['query_text']} target={row['target_text']!r} "
   268	                  f"packed={row['packed_text']!r} single={row['single_text']!r} "
   269	                  f"same={row['packed_equals_single']}", flush=True)
   270	    if n_bad:
   271	        sys.exit(2)

   274	def million(out, model, tables, host, arch, symbols):
   275	    cert = fwd.measure_invariance(model, tables, widths=(1,), row_limit=1, include_chunk=False)
   276	    if b1_bad(cert):
   277	        atomic_json(out / "meta.json", {"seed": SEED, "host": host, "b1_ok": False})
   278	        sys.exit(2)
   279	    a, b = alg.draw_instances(SEED, 1000000, 1)[0]
   280	    got = addition_record(model, tables, a, b, None)
   281	    atomic_json(out / "a.json", {"a": a})
   282	    atomic_json(out / "b.json", {"b": b})
   283	    atomic_json(out / "hs_sum.json", {"hs_sum": got["hs_sum"]})
   284	    atomic_json(out / "bl_sum.json", {"bl_sum": got["bl_sum"]})
   285	    meta = {k: v for k, v in got.items() if k not in ("a", "b", "hs_sum", "bl_sum", "bigint")}
   286	    meta.update({"seed": SEED, "host": host, "n_digits": 1000000, "n_instances": 1, "i": 0,
   287	                 "bigint_len": len(got["bigint"]), "hs_len": len(got["hs_sum"] or ""),
   288	                 "arch": arch})
   289	    atomic_json(out / "meta.json", meta)
   290	    print(f"million exact={got['hs_exact'] and got['bl_exact']} hs_s={got['hs_model_seconds']:.2f}", flush=True)

   292	
   293	def main():
   294	    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser()
   295	    ap.add_argument("--mode", required=True,
   296	                    choices=("smoke", "counted", "l4smoke", "l4", "million", "probe", "diag", "search"))
   297	    ap.add_argument("--symbol-ids", default="",
   298	                    help="Comma-separated token ids already selected by a finished search. Skips the walk.")
   299	    ap.add_argument("--out", required=True)
   300	    args = ap.parse_args()
   301	    out = __import__("pathlib").Path(args.out)
   302	    out.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
   303	    try:
   304	        model, tok, host, arch = load_runtime()
   305	        # Probe and diag inspect the lex-smallest triple. Every other mode walks the cosine order
   306	        # and keeps the first triple whose D, M, and S rows pass packed B=1.
   307	        if args.mode in ("probe", "diag"):
   308	            symbols = fwd.choose_symbols(model, tok)
   309	            trace = None
   310	        elif args.symbol_ids.strip():
   311	            symbols = fwd.symbols_from_ids(model, tok, args.symbol_ids.split(","))
   312	            trace = None
   313	            print(f"pinned symbols={symbols['text']} max_cos={symbols['max_pairwise_cosine']}", flush=True)
   314	        else:
   315	            def publish(trace, chosen=None):
   316	                atomic_json(out / "search.json", {
   317	                    "seed": SEED, "mode": args.mode, "host": host, "arch": arch, "chosen": chosen, **trace,
   318	                })
   319	            symbols, trace = fwd.search_certified_symbols(model, tok, on_checkpoint=publish)
   320	            publish(trace, symbols)
   321	            if symbols is None:
   322	                print(f"search found no triple stopped={trace['stopped_because']} tried={trace['n_tried']}",
   323	                      flush=True)
   324	                sys.exit(2)
   325	        tables = fwd.build_tables(symbols)
   326	        print(f"symbols={symbols['text']} max_cos={symbols['max_pairwise_cosine']}", flush=True)
   327	        if args.mode == "search":
   328	            print(f"search chose rank={trace['chosen_rank']} tried={trace['n_tried']}", flush=True)
   329	        elif args.mode == "probe":
   330	            probe(out, model, tok, tables, host, arch, symbols)
   331	        elif args.mode == "diag":
   332	            rec = fwd.diagnose_row(model, tok, tables)
   333	            rec.update({"seed": SEED, "host": host, "symbols": symbols["text"]})
   334	            atomic_json(out / "diag.json", rec)
   335	            print(f"diag rank={rec['target_rank']} top={rec['top'][:3]} "
   336	                  f"repeats={ {k: v['misses'] for k, v in rec['repeats'].items()} }", flush=True)
   337	        else:
   338	            {"smoke": smoke, "counted": counted, "l4smoke": l4_smoke, "l4": l4_time, "million": million}[args.mode](
   339	                out, model, tables, host, arch, symbols)
   340	    except SystemExit:
   341	        raise
   342	    except Exception:
   343	        atomic_json(out / "error.json", {"error": traceback.format_exc()[-4000:]})
   344	        raise
   345

   348	    main()

   772	def _consume(summary, expect, stats):
   773	    """summary is a list of (argmax, margin) aligned with (low, high) keys in `keys`."""
   774	    out_ids = []
   775	    for (arg, margin), key in zip(summary, expect):
   776	        target = key
   777	        match = target is not None and arg == target and margin > 0
   778	        if target is None:
   779	            stats["n_missing"] += 1
   780	        _note(stats, margin, match, {"arg": arg, "target": target, "margin": margin})
   781	        out_ids.append(arg)
   782	    return out_ids

   787	    _add_seconds(stats, pieces)

   791	def run_schedule(model, past, values, rounds, original, identity_id, expect, stats, chunk):
   792	    """`expect` maps (low_id, high_id) to the output id Π says. The array follows the model's argmax."""
   793	    a = list(values)
   794	    original = list(original)
   795	    P = past.get_seq_length()
   796	    for rnd in rounds:
   797	        actions = alg.plan_round(a, original, rnd)
   798	        lookups = [act for act in actions if act[0] == "lookup"]
   799	        summary = []
   800	        if lookups:
   801	            blocks = [[low, high] for _, _, low, high in lookups]
   802	            summary, pieces = chunked_packed(model, past, P, blocks, chunk)
   803	            _add_seconds(stats, pieces)
   804	        new = list(a)
   805	        li = 0
   806	        for act in actions:
   807	            if act[0] == "lookup":
   808	                _, dest, low, high = act
   809	                arg, margin = summary[li]
   810	                li += 1
   811	                target = expect.get((low, high))
   812	                match = target is not None and arg == target and margin > 0
   813	                if target is None:
   814	                    stats["n_missing"] += 1
   815	                if not match and len(stats["bad"]) < 8:
   816	                    stats["bad"].append({"low": low, "high": high, "arg": arg, "target": target, "margin": margin})
   817	                _note_silent(stats, margin, match)
   818	                new[dest] = arg
   819	            elif act[0] == "value":
   820	                _, dest, value = act
   821	                new[dest] = value
   822	            elif act[0] == "identity":
   823	                new[act[1]] = identity_id
   824	        a = new
   825	    return a

   838	def carries_from_prefixes(prefix_ids, g_id, zero_id, one_id):
   839	    cin_ids = []
   840	    cout_ids = []
   841	    cin = zero_id
   842	    for p in prefix_ids:
   843	        cin_ids.append(cin)
   844	        cout = one_id if p == g_id else zero_id
   845	        cout_ids.append(cout)
   846	        cin = cout
   847	    final = 1 if cin == one_id else 0
   848	    return cin_ids, cout_ids, final

   851	def sum_from_digit_ids(digit_ids, final_carry, id_to_digit):
   852	    chars = []
   853	    for i in digit_ids:
   854	        if i not in id_to_digit:
   855	            return None
   856	        chars.append(str(id_to_digit[i]))
   857	    if final_carry:
   858	        chars.append("1")
   859	    text = "".join(reversed(chars)).lstrip("0")
   860	    return text or "0"

   863	def run_addition(model, tables, a, b, margin_floor=None, m_table=None):
   864	    """One addition. D once, then Hillis–Steele and Blelloch, then S for each schedule's carries.
   865	
   866	    `m_table` replaces M (a corrupted prefix). Lookups are scored against that table, so a corrupted row
   867	    matches when the model copies the corrupted prefix.
   868	    """
   869	    sym = tables["symbols"]
   870	    dig = sym["digit_ids"]
   871	    id_to_digit = {i: d for d, i in enumerate(dig)}
   872	    g_id = sym["id"]["G"]
   873	    pairs = alg.columns(a, b)
   874	    n = len(pairs)
   875	    stats = {"D": new_stats(), "HS": new_stats(), "BL": new_stats(), "S_HS": new_stats(), "S_BL": new_stats()}
   876	
   877	    d_table = tables["D"]
   878	    past_d = prefill(model, d_table["pi"])
   879	    d_blocks = [[dig[x], dig[y]] for x, y in pairs]
   880	    d_targets = [d_table["expect"].get(tuple(b)) for b in d_blocks]
   881	    d_ids = lookup_blocks(model, past_d, len(d_table["pi"]), d_blocks, d_targets, stats["D"], chunk_for(2))
   882	    del past_d
   883	
   884	    def one_scan(kind, rounds, values):
   885	        m = tables["M"] if m_table is None else m_table
   886	        past = prefill(model, m["pi"])
   887	        padded_n = len(values)
   888	        original = list(values)
   889	        got = run_schedule(model, past, values, rounds, original, sym["id"]["P"], m["expect"], stats[kind], chunk_for(2))
   890	        del past
   891	        return got[:n] if padded_n != n else got
   892	
   893	    hs_rounds = alg.hillis_schedule(n)
   894	    hs_ids = one_scan("HS", hs_rounds, list(d_ids))
   895	    pad, bl_rounds = alg.blelloch_schedule(n)
   896	    bl_values = list(d_ids) + [sym["id"]["P"]] * (pad - n)
   897	    bl_ids = one_scan("BL", bl_rounds, bl_values)
   898	
   899	    def s_round(prefix_ids, label):
   900	        # The carry into column 0 is 0. The carry into a later column is the scan prefix's carry.
   901	        # The cout token in the S query is the adder row's own cout for that (cin, a, b), so the query
   902	        # addresses a real row. On a correct monoid this cout equals the prefix's carry.
   903	        cin_bits = []
   904	        cin = 0
   905	        scan_cout = []
   906	        for p in prefix_ids:
   907	            cin_bits.append(cin)
   908	            bit = 1 if p == g_id else 0
   909	            scan_cout.append(bit)
   910	            cin = bit
   911	        final = cin
   912	        s = tables["S"]
   913	        past = prefill(model, s["pi"])
   914	        blocks = []
   915	        targets = []
   916	        adder_cout = []
   917	        for i, (x, y) in enumerate(pairs):
   918	            cout = 1 if cin_bits[i] + x + y >= 10 else 0
   919	            adder_cout.append(cout)
   920	            block = [dig[cin_bits[i]], dig[x], dig[y], dig[cout]]
   921	            blocks.append(block)
   922	            targets.append(s["expect"].get(tuple(block)))
   923	        outs = lookup_blocks(model, past, len(s["pi"]), blocks, targets, stats[label], chunk_for(4))
   924	        del past
   925	        text = sum_from_digit_ids(outs, final, id_to_digit)
   926	        return text, final, scan_cout == adder_cout
   927	
   928	    hs_sum, hs_final, hs_carry_ok = s_round(hs_ids, "S_HS")
   929	    bl_sum, bl_final, bl_carry_ok = s_round(bl_ids, "S_BL")
   930	    truth = alg.bigint_sum(a, b)
   931	    margin_holds = True
   932	    if margin_floor is not None:
   933	        for st in stats.values():
   934	            if st["n"] and (st["min_margin"] is None or st["min_margin"] <= margin_floor):
   935	                margin_holds = False
   936	    return {
   937	        "n_digits": n,
   938	        "hs_sum": hs_sum,
   939	        "bl_sum": bl_sum,
   940	        "bigint": truth,
   941	        "hs_exact": hs_sum == truth,

   955	def light_on_model(model, corrupted, sym_ids):
   956	    """27 triples as model lookups against one corrupted M. Two waves: the inner products, then the outer ones."""
   957	    past = prefill(model, corrupted["pi"])
   958	    P = len(corrupted["pi"])
   959	    names = ("K", "P", "G")
   960	    triples = [(x, y, z) for x in names for y in names for z in names]
   961	    id_of = sym_ids
   962	    wave1 = []
   963	    for x, y, z in triples:
   964	        wave1.append([id_of[x], id_of[y]])
   965	        wave1.append([id_of[y], id_of[z]])
   966	    got1, pieces1 = chunked_packed(model, past, P, wave1, chunk_for(2))
   967	    wave2 = []
   968	    for t, (x, y, z) in enumerate(triples):
   969	        wave2.append([got1[2 * t][0], id_of[z]])
   970	        wave2.append([id_of[x], got1[2 * t + 1][0]])
   971	    got2, pieces2 = chunked_packed(model, past, P, wave2, chunk_for(2))
   972	    fails = []
   973	    n_match = 0
   974	    n = 0
   975	    min_margin = None
   976	    for t, (x, y, z) in enumerate(triples):
   977	        left_id = got2[2 * t][0]
   978	        right_id = got2[2 * t + 1][0]
   979	        quads = (
   980	            (got1[2 * t], (id_of[x], id_of[y])),
   981	            (got1[2 * t + 1], (id_of[y], id_of[z])),
   982	            (got2[2 * t], (got1[2 * t][0], id_of[z])),
   983	            (got2[2 * t + 1], (id_of[x], got1[2 * t + 1][0])),
   984	        )
   985	        for (arg, margin), (low, high) in quads:
   986	            n += 1
   987	            min_margin = margin if min_margin is None else min(min_margin, margin)
   988	            target = corrupted["expect"].get((low, high))
   989	            n_match += int(target is not None and arg == target and margin > 0)
   990	        if left_id != right_id:
   991	            fails.append(x + y + z)
   992	    del past
   993	    return {"fails": fails, "n_fail": len(fails), "n_lookups": n, "n_match_pi": n_match,
   994	            "min_margin": min_margin, "seconds": sum(pieces1) + sum(pieces2),
   995	            "chunk_seconds": pieces1 + pieces2}
   996

codex
The certificate uses the correct factor of two: minimum margin must exceed twice the maximum logit difference. Its coverage is empirical, though. I also found that the report’s 4,096-digit timings are around 3.5 seconds, not 2.2, and measure forward time rather than end-to-end time. I’m checking the saved operands and outputs independently next.

   305	        # Probe and diag inspect the lex-smallest triple. Every other mode walks the cosine order

   258	def probe(out, model, tok, tables, host, arch, symbols):
   259	    rec = with_pi(common("probe", host, arch, symbols), tables)
   260	    rec["tables"] = [fwd.probe_table(model, tok, tables[name], name) for name in ("D", "M", "S")]

   265	        print(f"  {t['table']} ok={t['n_ok']}/{t['n']} min_margin={t['min_margin']}", flush=True)
   266	        for row in t["bad"][:8]:

   268	                  f"packed={row['packed_text']!r} single={row['single_text']!r} "
   269	                  f"same={row['packed_equals_single']}", flush=True)
   270	    if n_bad:

    63	def name_pool(tok, n_train=240, n_test=120):
    64	    """Disjoint train and test names; every name stays one token beside every other name and beside a newline,
    65	    so a row's token ids are exactly (s, e, t, newline) and a block's are (s, e). Each split keeps the kinds'
    66	    proportions of the candidate list."""
    67	    nl = _ids(tok, "\n")
    68	    assert len(nl) == 1, "newline is not one token"
    69	    nl = nl[0]
    70	    cands = _candidates(tok)
    71	    cut = int(0.66 * len(cands))
    72	    id_of, pools = {}, {}
    73	    for split, src, n in (("train", cands[:cut], n_train), ("test", cands[cut:], n_test)):
    74	        chosen = []
    75	        for w, _kind in src:
    76	            if len(chosen) >= n:
    77	                break
    78	            wid = _ids(tok, " " + w)[0]
    79	            if w in id_of or wid in id_of.values():
    80	                continue
    81	            id_of[w] = wid
    82	            if _fits(tok, w, wid, [c for c in id_of if c != w], id_of, nl):
    83	                chosen.append(w)
    84	            else:
    85	                del id_of[w]
    86	        pools[split] = chosen
    87	    # Family alphabets the clock will walk by id: bare digits (Qwen splits digits, so " 1" is two tokens), single
    88	    # letters with a space, brackets. They are names by id only (no text round trip), and only in training.
    89	    for name, text in ([(d, d) for d in "0123456789"] + [(c, " " + c) for c in "ABCDEFGHIJKLMNOPQRSTUVWXYZabcdefghijklmnopqrstuvwxyz"]
    90	                       + [(b, b) for b in "()[]"]):
    91	        ids = _ids(tok, text)
    92	        if len(ids) == 1 and ids[0] not in id_of.values() and name not in id_of:
    93	            id_of[name] = ids[0]
    94	            pools["train"].append(name)
    95	    kinds = dict(cands)
    96	    return {"train": pools["train"], "test": pools["test"], "id_of": id_of, "nl": nl,
    97	            "kinds": {s: {k: sum(kinds.get(w) == k for w in pools[s]) for k in ("lower", "cap", "code", "letter")}
    98	                      | {"extra": sum(w not in kinds for w in pools[s])} for s in ("train", "test")}}
    99	
   100

   209	def prefill(model, pi_ids):
   210	    import torch
   211	    ids = torch.tensor([pi_ids], device=model.device)
   212	    pos = torch.arange(len(pi_ids), device=model.device)[None]
   213	    with torch.no_grad():
   214	        return model(input_ids=ids, position_ids=pos, use_cache=True).past_key_values
   215	
   216	
   217	def _clone(past):
   218	    import copy
   219	    import torch
   220	    if hasattr(past, "layers"):
   221	        new = copy.copy(past)
   222	        new.layers = []
   223	        for layer in past.layers:
   224	            c = copy.copy(layer)
   225	            for attr in ("keys", "values"):
   226	                v = getattr(c, attr, None)
   227	                if torch.is_tensor(v):
   228	                    setattr(c, attr, v.clone())
   229	            new.layers.append(c)
   230	        return new
   231	    if hasattr(past, "key_cache"):
   232	        new = copy.copy(past)
   233	        new.key_cache = [t.clone() for t in past.key_cache]
   234	        new.value_cache = [t.clone() for t in past.value_cache]
   235	        return new
   236	    return tuple(tuple(t.clone() for t in layer) for layer in past)
   237	
   238	
   239	def tick(model, past, P, s_id, e_id):
   240	    """One tick: the block [s][e] at positions P, P+1 over a copy of Π's cache. Returns the full-vocabulary
   241	    logits at the event position (the copy is discarded, so Π's cache is never written)."""
   242	    import torch
   243	    pos = torch.tensor([[P, P + 1]], device=model.device)
   244	    with torch.no_grad():
   245	        out = model(input_ids=torch.tensor([[s_id, e_id]], device=model.device), position_ids=pos,
   246	                    cache_position=pos[0], past_key_values=_clone(past), use_cache=True)
   247	    return out.logits[0, -1]
   248	
   249	
   415	def merge_adapter(model_name, adapter, dest):
   416	    import torch
   417	    from peft import PeftModel
   418	    from transformers import AutoModelForCausalLM, AutoTokenizer
   419	    base = AutoModelForCausalLM.from_pretrained(model_name, dtype=torch.bfloat16)
   420	    merged = PeftModel.from_pretrained(base, adapter).merge_and_unload()
   421	    merged.save_pretrained(dest, safe_serialization=True)
   422	    AutoTokenizer.from_pretrained(model_name).save_pretrained(dest)
   423	    del base, merged
   424	    gc.collect()

    23	def host_record():
    24	    """CPU model, AVX-512F flag and GPU name of the machine this runs on. Goes into every record."""
    25	    info = {"gpu": None, "gpu_count": 0, "cpu": None, "cpu_vendor_family_model": None, "avx512f": None,
    26	            "platform": platform.platform(), "hostname_hash": sha256_bytes(platform.node().encode())[:12]}
    27	    try:
    28	        import torch
    29	        if torch.cuda.is_available():
    30	            info["gpu"] = torch.cuda.get_device_name(0)
    31	            info["gpu_count"] = torch.cuda.device_count()
    32	    except Exception:
    33	        pass
    34	    if info["gpu"] is None:
    35	        try:
    36	            out = subprocess.check_output(["nvidia-smi", "--query-gpu=name", "--format=csv,noheader"], text=True, timeout=10)
    37	            names = [x.strip() for x in out.splitlines() if x.strip()]
    38	            if names:
    39	                info["gpu"], info["gpu_count"] = names[0], len(names)
    40	        except Exception:
    41	            info["gpu"] = "none"
    42	    try:
    43	        text = pathlib.Path("/proc/cpuinfo").read_text()
    44	        fields = {}
    45	        for line in text.splitlines():
    46	            if ":" in line:
    47	                key, val = line.split(":", 1)
    48	                fields.setdefault(key.strip().lower(), val.strip())
    49	        info["cpu"] = fields.get("model name")
    50	        info["cpu_vendor_family_model"] = " ".join(
    51	            str(fields.get(k)) for k in ("vendor_id", "cpu family", "model", "stepping"))
    52	        info["avx512f"] = "avx512f" in fields.get("flags", "").split()
    53	        info["cpu_count"] = os.cpu_count()
    54	    except OSError:
    55	        try:
    56	            info["cpu"] = subprocess.check_output(["sysctl", "-n", "machdep.cpu.brand_string"], text=True, timeout=10).strip()
    57	        except Exception:
    58	            info["cpu"] = platform.processor() or None
    59	        info["avx512f"] = False
    60	        info["cpu_count"] = os.cpu_count()
    61	    return info
    62

   128	def atomic_json(path, value):
   129	    path = pathlib.Path(path)
   130	    path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
   131	    tmp = path.with_suffix(path.suffix + f".{os.getpid()}.tmp")
   132	    tmp.write_text(json.dumps(value, indent=1, sort_keys=True, default=str))
   133	    os.replace(tmp, path)

    22	if hasattr(sys, "set_int_max_str_digits"):
    23	    sys.set_int_max_str_digits(0)

    25	K, P, G = "K", "P", "G"
    26	SYMBOLS = (K, P, G)
    27	# Token ids are assigned later, smallest id first. This order is the algebra's order.
    28	NAME_ORDER = {K: 0, P: 1, G: 2}
    29	
    30	
    31	def classify(a, b):
    32	    s = a + b
    33	    if s <= 8:
    34	        return K
    35	    if s == 9:
    36	        return P
    37	    return G
    38	
    39	
    40	def combine(low, high):
    41	    """Monoid product. `low` is the less significant block, `high` the more significant one."""
    42	    if high == P:
    43	        return low
    44	    if high in (K, G):
    45	        return high
    46	    raise ValueError(high)
    47	
    48	
    49	def monoid_rows():
    50	    return [(low, high, combine(low, high)) for low in SYMBOLS for high in SYMBOLS]
    51	
    52	
    53	def digit_rows():
    54	    return [(a, b, classify(a, b)) for a in range(10) for b in range(10)]
    55	
    56	
    57	def adder_rows():
    58	    """(c, a, b, c', s) with c' * 10 + s = c + a + b. Same rows as arith_core.adder_rows."""
    59	    rows = []
    60	    for c in range(2):
    61	        for a in range(10):
    62	            for b in range(10):
    63	                cout, s = divmod(c + a + b, 10)
    64	                rows.append((c, a, b, cout, s))
    65	    return rows
    66	
    67	
    68	def apply_table(table, low, high):
    69	    return table[(low, high)]
    70	
    71	
    72	def true_table():
    73	    return {(low, high): out for low, high, out in monoid_rows()}
    74	
    75	
    76	def light_failures(op):
    77	    """Light's associativity test: the triples (x, y, z) with (x*y)*z != x*(y*z)."""
    78	    fails = []
    79	    for x in SYMBOLS:
    80	        for y in SYMBOLS:
    81	            for z in SYMBOLS:
    82	                left = op(op(x, y), z)
    83	                right = op(x, op(y, z))
    84	                if left != right:
    85	                    fails.append((x, y, z))
    86	    return fails
    87	
    88	
    89	def corruptions():
    90	    """The 18 single-row corruptions: each of 9 rows replaced by each of the 2 wrong symbols."""
    91	    base = true_table()
    92	    out = []
    93	    for low in SYMBOLS:
    94	        for high in SYMBOLS:
    95	            truth = base[(low, high)]
    96	            for wrong in SYMBOLS:
    97	                if wrong == truth:
    98	                    continue
    99	                table = dict(base)
   100	                table[(low, high)] = wrong
   101	                fails = light_failures(lambda x, y, table=table: table[(x, y)])
   102	                out.append({
   103	                    "low": low,
   104	                    "high": high,
   105	                    "true": truth,
   106	                    "wrong": wrong,
   107	                    "table": {f"{a}{b}": table[(a, b)] for a in SYMBOLS for b in SYMBOLS},
   108	                    "fails": ["".join(t) for t in fails],
   109	                    "n_fail": len(fails),
   110	                    "detectable": len(fails) > 0,
   111	                })
   112	    return out
   113	
   114	
   127	def hillis_schedule(n):
   128	    """Inclusive Hillis–Steele. Each round is a list of ('lookup', dest, low_index, high_index).
   129	
   130	    Reads are from the array at the start of the round. Round count is ceil(log2 n) for n >= 1
   131	    (0 when n <= 1).
   132	    """
   133	    rounds = []
   134	    offset = 1
   135	    while offset < n:
   136	        rounds.append([("lookup", i, i - offset, i) for i in range(offset, n)])
   137	        offset *= 2
   138	    return rounds
   139	
   140	
   141	def blelloch_schedule(n):
   142	    """Inclusive Blelloch on the next power of two, padded with the identity P.
   143	
   144	    Returns (pad_length, rounds). Rounds may contain
   145	    ('lookup', dest, i, j), ('copy', dest, src), ('set_identity', index),
   146	    ('lookup_orig', dest, excl_index).
   147	    'lookup_orig' multiplies the exclusive value by the ORIGINAL padded element.
   148	    'set_identity' is not a model lookup.
   149	    """
   150	    pad = 1
   151	    while pad < n:
   152	        pad *= 2
   153	    rounds = []
   154	    logn = pad.bit_length() - 1
   155	    for d in range(logn):
   156	        step = 1 << (d + 1)
   157	        half = 1 << d
   158	        ops = []
   159	        for k in range(0, pad, step):
   160	            left = k + half - 1
   161	            right = k + step - 1
   162	            ops.append(("lookup", right, left, right))
   163	        rounds.append(ops)
   164	    rounds.append([("set_identity", pad - 1)])
   165	    for d in range(logn - 1, -1, -1):
   166	        step = 1 << (d + 1)
   167	        half = 1 << d
   168	        ops = []
   169	        for k in range(0, pad, step):
   170	            left = k + half - 1
   171	            right = k + step - 1
   172	            ops.append(("copy", left, right))
   173	            # Down-sweep: the right child must become (parent exclusive) ⊕ (left reduction).
   174	            # Parent exclusive sits at `right`, left reduction at `left`. The operator does not commute,
   175	            # so the low operand is `right` and the high operand is `left`. The copy above reads the snapshot.
   176	            ops.append(("lookup", right, right, left))
   177	        rounds.append(ops)
   178	    rounds.append([("lookup_orig", i, i) for i in range(pad)])
   179	    return pad, rounds
   180	
   181	
   182	def attention_allows(P, n_blocks, block_len, q, k):
   183	    """True when packed query `q` may attend to key `k`.
   184	
   185	    Keys 0..P-1 are Π. Keys P.. are the blocks, `block_len` each. Query q attends to every Π key and to the
   186	    keys of its own block up to itself. This is the pinned tick's causal pattern when n_blocks is 1.
   187	    """
   188	    Q = n_blocks * block_len
   189	    if not (0 <= q < Q) or k < 0 or k >= P + Q:
   190	        return False
   191	    if k < P:
   192	        return True
   193	    bq = q // block_len
   194	    bk = (k - P) // block_len
   195	    if bq != bk:
   196	        return False
   197	    return (k - P) % block_len <= q % block_len
   198	
   199	
   200	def allow_count(P, n_blocks, block_len):
   201	    Q = n_blocks * block_len
   202	    return sum(1 for q in range(Q) for k in range(P + Q) if attention_allows(P, n_blocks, block_len, q, k))
   203	
   204	
   205	def model_rounds(rounds):
   206	    """Rounds that contain at least one model lookup."""
   207	    return [r for r in rounds if any(op[0] in ("lookup", "lookup_orig") for op in r)]
   208	
   209	
   210	def plan_round(old, original, rnd):
   211	    """Reads for one round, taken from the snapshot `old` (and `original` for the inclusive step).
   212	
   213	    Each action is ('lookup', dest, low, high) or ('value', dest, value) or ('identity', dest).
   214	    The model runner and `eval_schedule` both consume this, so the index arithmetic lives in one place.
   215	    """
   216	    actions = []
   217	    for opnd in rnd:
   218	        kind = opnd[0]
   219	        if kind == "lookup":
   220	            _, dest, i, j = opnd
   221	            actions.append(("lookup", dest, old[i], old[j]))
   222	        elif kind == "lookup_orig":
   223	            _, dest, i = opnd
   224	            actions.append(("lookup", dest, old[i], original[i]))
   225	        elif kind == "copy":
   226	            _, dest, src = opnd
   227	            actions.append(("value", dest, old[src]))
   228	        elif kind == "set_identity":
   229	            actions.append(("identity", opnd[1]))
   230	        else:
   231	            raise ValueError(kind)
   232	    return actions
   233	
   234	
   286	def random_number(rng, n):
   287	    """Exactly n digits. A 1-digit draw may be 0; longer draws have no leading zero.
   288	    Same rule as arith_data.random_number."""
   289	    if n == 1:
   290	        return str(rng.randrange(0, 10))
   291	    return str(rng.randrange(1, 10)) + "".join(str(rng.randrange(10)) for _ in range(n - 1))
   292	
   293	
   294	def draw_instances(seed, n_digits, n):
   295	    rng = random.Random(seed)
   296	    return [(random_number(rng, n_digits), random_number(rng, n_digits)) for _ in range(n)]
   297	
   298	
   299	def columns(a, b):
   300	    """Least-significant-first digit pairs. Both strings are used as written (no extra pair)."""
   301	    n = max(len(a), len(b))
   302	    ra, rb = a[::-1].ljust(n, "0"), b[::-1].ljust(n, "0")
   303	    return [(int(ra[i]), int(rb[i])) for i in range(n)]
   304	
   305	
   306	def prefixes_of(pairs, op=combine):
   307	    blocks = [classify(a, b) for a, b in pairs]
   308	    return blocks, sequential_prefix(blocks, op)
   309	
   310	
   311	def digits_from_prefixes(pairs, prefixes):
   312	    """Sum digits, least-significant first, and the final carry. Carry-in to column 0 is 0.
   313	    Carry-out of a prefix is 1 exactly when the prefix is G."""
   314	    cin = 0
   315	    digits = []
   316	    cins = []
   317	    for i, (a, b) in enumerate(pairs):
   318	        cins.append(cin)
   319	        digits.append((cin + a + b) % 10)
   320	        cin = 1 if prefixes[i] == G else 0
   321	    return digits, cins, cin
   322	
   323	
   324	def number_from_digits(digits, final_carry):
   325	    chars = [str(d) for d in digits]
   326	    if final_carry:
   327	        chars.append("1")
   328	    text = "".join(reversed(chars)).lstrip("0")
   329	    return text or "0"
   330	
   331	
   332	def add_with(a, b, op=combine):
   333	    pairs = columns(a, b)
   334	    blocks, prefixes = prefixes_of(pairs, op)
   335	    digits, cins, final = digits_from_prefixes(pairs, prefixes)
   336	    return {
   337	        "pairs": pairs,
   338	        "blocks": blocks,
   339	        "prefixes": prefixes,
   340	        "digits": digits,
   341	        "cins": cins,
   342	        "final_carry": final,
   343	        "sum": number_from_digits(digits, final),
   344	    }
   345	
   346	
   347	def bigint_sum(a, b):
   348	    return str(int(a) + int(b))
   349

   535	def demo_choice(seed, n_digits, n_inst):
   536	    """The planted corruption: lexicographically smallest detectable corruption, under K<P<G,
   537	    whose failing-triple signature is unique among the 18, and which changes instance 0's sum.
   538	    """
   539	    corrs = corruptions()
   540	    groups = signature_groups(corrs)
   541	    inst = draw_instances(seed, n_digits, n_inst)[0]
   542	    true_sum = bigint_sum(*inst)
   543	    options = []
   544	    for c in corrs:
   545	        if not c["detectable"]:
   546	            continue
   547	        sig = tuple(c["fails"])
   548	        if len(groups[sig]) != 1:
   549	            continue
   550	        table = {(a, b): c["table"][f"{a}{b}"] for a in SYMBOLS for b in SYMBOLS}
   551	        bad = add_with(inst[0], inst[1], lambda x, y, table=table: table[(x, y)])
   552	        if bad["sum"] == true_sum:
   553	            continue
   554	        options.append((NAME_ORDER[c["low"]], NAME_ORDER[c["high"]], NAME_ORDER[c["wrong"]], c, bad["sum"]))
   555	    if not options:
   556	        return None
   557	    options.sort(key=lambda t: t[:3])
   558	    _, _, _, c, bad_sum = options[0]
   559	    blocks = [classify(a, b) for a, b in columns(inst[0], inst[1])]
   560	    table = {(a, b): c["table"][f"{a}{b}"] for a in SYMBOLS for b in SYMBOLS}
   561	    where = affected_columns(blocks, table, c["low"], c["high"])
   562	    return {
   563	        "low": c["low"],
   564	        "high": c["high"],
   565	        "true": c["true"],
   566	        "wrong": c["wrong"],
   567	        "fails": c["fails"],
   568	        "instance0_true_sum_sha256": hashlib.sha256(true_sum.encode()).hexdigest(),
   569	        "instance0_bad_sum_sha256": hashlib.sha256(bad_sum.encode()).hexdigest(),
   570	        "sums_differ": true_sum != bad_sum,
   571	        "where": {k: where[k] for k in ("n_used", "n_prefix_diff", "n_cin_diff")},
   572	        "cin_diff_equals_used": where["cin_diff"] == where["used_columns"],
   573	    }

   585	def necessity_inputs():
   586	    """One 2-column input per monoid row.
   587	
   588	    A wrong output changes some sum exactly when it leaves the carry class of the true output
   589	    (G versus K or P). Swapping K with P does not change any sum: both emit carry 0, and every
   590	    right-multiplication preserves that agreement. See the theory note.
   591	    """
   592	    force = {K: (1, 1), P: (9, 0), G: (5, 5)}
   593	    high_pair = {K: (1, 0), P: (4, 5), G: (9, 1)}
   594	    out = []
   595	    base = true_table()
   596	    for low in SYMBOLS:
   597	        for high in SYMBOLS:
   598	            a0, b0 = force[low]
   599	            a1, b1 = high_pair[high]
   600	            a = str(a0 + 10 * a1)
   601	            b = str(b0 + 10 * b1)
   602	            blocks = [classify(a0, b0), classify(a1, b1)]
   603	            assert blocks == [low, high], blocks
   604	            uses = sequential_uses(blocks, combine)
   605	            assert uses == [(1, low, high)]
   606	            true_sum = bigint_sum(a, b)
   607	            wrongs = []
   608	            for wrong in SYMBOLS:
   609	                if wrong == base[(low, high)]:
   610	                    continue
   611	                table = dict(base)
   612	                table[(low, high)] = wrong
   613	                bad = add_with(a, b, lambda x, y, table=table: table[(x, y)])
   614	                changes = bad["sum"] != true_sum
   615	                crosses = not same_carry_class(base[(low, high)], wrong)
   616	                wrongs.append({
   617	                    "wrong": wrong, "sum": bad["sum"], "changes": changes, "crosses_carry_class": crosses,
   618	                })
   619	            out.append({
   620	                "low": low, "high": high, "a": a, "b": b, "true_sum": true_sum,
   621	                "wrongs": wrongs,
   622	                "every_carry_crossing_changes": all(w["changes"] for w in wrongs if w["crosses_carry_class"]),
   623	                "no_same_class_changes": all(not w["changes"] for w in wrongs if not w["crosses_carry_class"]),
   624	            })
   625	    return out

     1	Registered before any Modal spend. Every count below was written by scan_report.py from results/algebra.json (scan_algebra.audit, seed 20261028).
     2	
     3	1. Every row of D (100), M (9) and S (200) certifies on the packed path: the argmax equals the row and the margin is positive. The bitwise-or-margin rule is fixed here and is not a predicted branch. If the invariance grid is bitwise equal (the maximum absolute logit difference is 0), the certificate is bitwise. Otherwise the certificate is a margin certificate, and it passes only when the minimum packed margin is greater than twice that maximum absolute difference. A margin certificate is an empirical bound.
     4	
     5	2. On seed 20261028, 16 of 16 additions of 4096 digits and 4 of 4 additions of 100000 digits are exact under both Hillis–Steele and Blelloch. Exact means the digit string equals Python's bigint sum, the two schedules agree on every carry, and every lookup's argmax equals its row. When the certificate is a margin certificate, every lookup's margin also clears the bound from item 1.
     6	
     7	3. Hillis–Steele uses ceil(log2 n)+2 model rounds, counting the D round and the S round. That is 14 rounds at 4096 digits, 19 at 100000 digits, and 22 at 1000000 digits.
     8	
     9	4. One Hillis–Steele addition of 100000 digits takes under 10 seconds of model time on an H100. Model time is the packed forwards of D, the Hillis–Steele lookups, and S. It excludes the merge, the certification grid, Blelloch, and the fault injection.
    10	
    11	5. The in-model Light test flags exactly the 14 corruptions whose stored tables fail Light's test, and none of the 4 it does not flag (KP->G, KG->K, GK->G, GP->K). The planted row is KK->G. Its failing triples are KKK, KGK.
    12	
    13	6. The three state tokens chosen for K, P and G have pairwise cosine at most 0.3.
    14	
    15	The 1000000-digit addition is attempted only if the settled ledger still has room under the $15 cap after the 100000-digit run. There is no prediction that it will be run.
    16	
    17	Closeout's sequential clock is not a comparison target. The reason is computed in the resolved-choices section from the closeout record on disk.

    71	def local_files():
    72	    files = [(p.name, p) for d in SOURCE_DIRS for p in sorted(d.glob(SOURCE_GLOB))]
    73	    if len({n for n, _ in files}) != len(files):
    74	        raise ValueError("a file name occurs in more than one source directory")
    75	    return files
    76

    78	def sources(commit=None):
    79	    if commit is None:
    80	        return {n: sha256_bytes(p.read_bytes()) for n, p in local_files()}
    81	    out = {}
    82	    for d in SOURCE_DIRS:
    83	        names = subprocess.check_output(["git", "ls-tree", "-r", "--name-only", commit, "--", "."], cwd=d, text=True)
    84	        for n in names.split():
    85	            if fnmatch.fnmatch(n, SOURCE_GLOB) and "/" not in n:
    86	                out[n] = sha256_bytes(subprocess.check_output(["git", "show", f"{commit}:./{n}"], cwd=d))
    87	    return out
