   193	def worker(job, expected_sources, vol_commit, shipped=None):
   194	    started = time.monotonic()
   195	    remote_root = pathlib.Path("/root/pb")
   196	    if shipped:
   197	        for name, text in shipped.items():
   198	            (remote_root / name).write_text(text)
   199	    actual = {str(p.relative_to(remote_root)): sha256_bytes(p.read_bytes())
   200	              for p in sorted(remote_root.glob(SOURCE_GLOB))}
   201	    if actual != expected_sources:
   202	        diff = {"missing": sorted(set(expected_sources) - set(actual)),
   203	                "extra": sorted(set(actual) - set(expected_sources)),
   204	                "differ": sorted(k for k in set(actual) & set(expected_sources) if actual[k] != expected_sources[k])}
   205	        return {"error": f"remote source hash mismatch: {json.dumps(diff)}", "wall": round(time.monotonic() - started, 3)}
   206	    out_dir = pathlib.Path("/vol") / job["id"]
   207	    out_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
   208	    log_path = out_dir / "job.log"
   209	    command = [sys.executable, "-u", str(remote_root / job["script"]), *job["argv"], "--out", str(out_dir)]
   210	    env = os.environ.copy()
   211	    env.update({"PB_JOB_ID": job["id"], "TOKENIZERS_PARALLELISM": "false", "HF_HUB_OFFLINE": "1"})
   212	    timed_out, last_commit = False, time.monotonic()
   213	    with log_path.open("w") as fout:
   214	        process = subprocess.Popen(command, cwd=remote_root, stdout=fout, stderr=subprocess.STDOUT, text=True,
   215	                                   start_new_session=True, env=env)
   216	        deadline, offset = time.monotonic() + job["max_seconds"], 0
   217	        while process.poll() is None:
   218	            remaining = deadline - time.monotonic()
   219	            if remaining <= 0:
   220	                timed_out = True
   221	                os.killpg(process.pid, signal.SIGKILL)
   222	                process.wait()
   223	                break
   224	            try:
   225	                process.wait(timeout=min(20, remaining))
   226	            except subprocess.TimeoutExpired:
   227	                pass
   228	            with log_path.open() as f:
   229	                f.seek(offset)
   230	                new = f.read()
   231	                offset = f.tell()
   232	            if new.strip():
   233	                print(f"[{job['id']}] {new.strip()[-3000:]}", flush=True)
   234	            if time.monotonic() - last_commit > 600:
   235	                with contextlib.suppress(Exception):
   236	                    vol_commit()
   237	                last_commit = time.monotonic()
   238	    text = log_path.read_text()
   239	    result = {"wall": round(time.monotonic() - started, 3), "log_tail": text[-12000:],
   240	              "python_version": sys.version.split()[0], "remote_completed_at": stamp()}
   241	    if timed_out:
   242	        result["error"] = f"subprocess exceeded {job['max_seconds']} seconds"
   243	    elif process.returncode != 0:
   244	        result["error"] = f"subprocess exit {process.returncode}: {text[-4000:]}"
   245	    files = {}
   246	    for p in sorted(out_dir.rglob("*.json")):
   247	        if p.stat().st_size < 2_000_000:
   248	            files[str(p.relative_to(out_dir))] = p.read_text()
   249	    files["job.log.json"] = json.dumps({"job_id": job["id"], "log": text[-300000:]})
   250	    result["files"] = files
   251	    try:
   252	        result["gpu_actual"] = subprocess.check_output(
   253	            ["nvidia-smi", "--query-gpu=name", "--format=csv,noheader"], text=True, timeout=5).strip()
   254	    except Exception:
   255	        result["gpu_actual"] = "none"
   256	    try:
   257	        info = pathlib.Path("/proc/cpuinfo").read_text()
   258	        cpu = [line.split(":", 1)[1].strip() for line in info.splitlines()
   259	               if line.lower().startswith(("model name", "vendor_id", "cpu family", "model\t"))]
   260	        flags = next((line.split(":", 1)[1].split() for line in info.splitlines() if line.startswith("flags")), [])

codex
The saved outputs match independent Python integer addition: all 16 short runs, all four 100k runs, and both million-digit outputs. The 14/18 fault count also matches an independent enumeration. I found a separate theory bug: the claim that K↔P corruptions never change a sum fails for the parallel scan. With `KK→P`, Hillis–Steele returns `3010` for `1009 + 1001`; the exact answer is `2010`.

   308	def search_certified_symbols(model, tok, limit=SEARCH_LIMIT, on_checkpoint=None):
   309	    """First triple in the cosine order whose D, M, and S rows all pass packed B=1.
   310	
   311	    Rank 0 is the triple `choose_symbols` returns. A triple that fails D is not asked about M.
   312	    The walk stops at the first triple that passes D, M, S, and the full invariance certificate.
   313	    `on_checkpoint` receives a partial trace every 200 triples so a killed job still leaves a record.
   314	    """

   237	def adder_table(tok):
   238	    d = digit_ids(tok)
   239	    return [tuple(d[int(x)] for x in r) for r in A.adder_rows()]

   242	def mealy_table(rng, tok, pool, split="train", kind=None):
   243	    """A total Mealy table over S × A × B as id rows (s, a, b, t, o), its start and its kind."""
   244	    d = digit_ids(tok)
   245	    io = pool["id_of"]
   246	    kind = kind or rng.choices(["adder", "edited", "digits", "names"], weights=[0.25, 0.20, 0.25, 0.30])[0]
   247	    edited = []
   248	    if kind in ("adder", "edited"):
   249	        rows = adder_table(tok)
   250	        start = d[0]
   251	        if kind == "edited":
   252	            k = rng.randint(1, 24)
   253	            idx = rng.sample(range(len(rows)), k)
   254	            rows = list(rows)
   300	def mealy_pinned_example(rng, tok, pool, split="train", blocks=16):
   301	    table = mealy_table(rng, tok, pool, split)
   302	    nl = A.nl_id(tok)
   303	    rows = table["rows"]
   304	    pick = []
   305	    if table["edited"]:
   306	        pick += [rows[i] for i in rng.choices(table["edited"], k=blocks // 2)]
   307	    pick += [rows[i] for i in rng.sample(range(len(rows)), min(blocks - len(pick), len(rows)))]
   308	    while len(pick) < blocks:
   309	        pick.append(rng.choice(rows))
   310	    return {"pi": mealy_pi(table, nl), "blocks": [r[:4] for r in pick], "labels": [r[3:] for r in pick],
   311	            "kind": table["kind"]}

   645	def _absorb(report, equal, delta, margin, argmax_ok):
   646	    report["n_compared"] += 1
   647	    report["n_equal"] += int(equal and delta == 0.0)
   648	    if delta > report["max_abs_delta"]:
   649	        report["max_abs_delta"] = delta
   650	    if margin is not None:
   651	        report["min_margin"] = margin if report["min_margin"] is None else min(report["min_margin"], margin)
   652	    if not argmax_ok:
   653	        report["n_argmax_mismatch"] += 1
   654	
   655	
   656	def _top(logit):
   657	    import torch
   658	    vals, idx = torch.topk(logit.float(), 2)
   659	    return int(idx[0]), float(vals[0] - vals[1])
   660	
   661	
   662	def _absorb_ends(report, ends, ref, target):
   663	    """Every slot of `ends` against one reference row. bf16->float32 is exact, so a 0 delta is bitwise equality."""
   664	    import torch
   665	    fails = 0
   666	    for i in range(0, ends.shape[0], 128):
   667	        part = ends[i:i + 128].float()
   668	        vals, idx = torch.topk(part, 2, dim=-1)
   669	        delta = (part - ref.float()).abs().amax(dim=-1)
   670	        for j in range(part.shape[0]):
   671	            arg = int(idx[j, 0])
   672	            margin = float(vals[j, 0] - vals[j, 1])
   673	            d = float(delta[j])
   674	            ok = arg == target and margin > 0
   675	            _absorb(report, d == 0.0, d, margin, ok)
   676	            fails += int(not ok)
   677	        del part
   678	    return fails
   679

2. **Frozen rules — partly OK; one latent BUG.**

   The frozen text is reproduced unchanged. It requires `"greater than twice that maximum absolute difference"` ([registered_predictions.md:3](/Users/neo/repos/polyformer-wt/scan/research/scan/results/registered_predictions.md:3)); code correctly uses `bound = 2.0 * report["max_abs_delta"]` ([scan_forward.py:750](/Users/neo/repos/polyformer-wt/scan/research/scan/scan_forward.py:750)). **Δ < margin alone is insufficient**: both winning and competing logits can move.

   Predictions 2, 3 and 5 match the saved results; prediction 6 matches saved cosines. Original prediction 4, `"under 10 seconds"`, **fails**; the amended **183.509-second** threshold passes. Original prediction 1 failed on the first triple; the report correctly calls the selected-triple result `"a retest"` ([registered_predictions.md:5](/Users/neo/repos/polyformer-wt/scan/research/scan/results/registered_predictions.md:5), [9](/Users/neo/repos/polyformer-wt/scan/research/scan/results/registered_predictions.md:9); [SCAN.md:43](/Users/neo/repos/polyformer-wt/scan/research/scan/SCAN.md:43), [128](/Users/neo/repos/polyformer-wt/scan/research/scan/SCAN.md:128)). The bitwise branch violates the row-correctness requirement; see bugs below.

3. **Leakage — DESIGN FLAW for a generalization interpretation.**

   Every query’s labeled table row is deliberately present in Π: `_pi` executes `pi.extend(row)`, while queries use `row[:block_len]`. Thus this demonstrates execution of supplied lookup tables, not unseen-label arithmetic ([scan_forward.py:225](/Users/neo/repos/polyformer-wt/scan/research/scan/scan_forward.py:225), [258](/Users/neo/repos/polyformer-wt/scan/research/scan/scan_forward.py:258)). The S table also appears in adapter training: `"adder"` selects `adder_table(tok)` ([arith_data.py:246](/Users/neo/repos/polyformer-wt/scan/research/arith/arith_data.py:246)).

   Host code additionally supplies S’s carry-out using `cin_bits[i] + x + y >= 10`; it is a hybrid algorithm ([scan_forward.py:918](/Users/neo/repos/polyformer-wt/scan/research/scan/scan_forward.py:918)). The complete bigint answer is computed **after** model outputs and compared with full-string equality, not fed into them ([scan_forward.py:928](/Users/neo/repos/polyformer-wt/scan/research/scan/scan_forward.py:928)). Symbol selection evaluates the `"test"` name pool, so that pool is selection data, not an untouched holdout ([scan_forward.py:136](/Users/neo/repos/polyformer-wt/scan/research/scan/scan_forward.py:136), [403](/Users/neo/repos/polyformer-wt/scan/research/scan/scan_forward.py:403)).

4. **Comparison fairness, isolation and timing.**

   - **OK — common model/data.** Both schedules consume the same `d_ids` and tables ([scan_forward.py:893](/Users/neo/repos/polyformer-wt/scan/research/scan/scan_forward.py:893)). They share `adapter_step500`, rank **64**, training LR **0.0001**, **500 steps**; pinned, Mealy and CE/KL losses run from the training loop’s beginning ([scan_forward.py:19](/Users/neo/repos/polyformer-wt/scan/research/scan/scan_forward.py:19); [adapter_config.json:31](/Users/neo/repos/polyformer-wt/scan/research/arith/results/arith-s1r2/adapter_step500/adapter_config.json:31); [train_record.json:12](/Users/neo/repos/polyformer-wt/scan/research/arith/results/arith-s1r2/train_record.json:12), [30](/Users/neo/repos/polyformer-wt/scan/research/arith/results/arith-s1r2/train_record.json:30); [arith_train.py:197](/Users/neo/repos/polyformer-wt/scan/research/arith/arith_train.py:197), [218](/Users/neo/repos/polyformer-wt/scan/research/arith/arith_train.py:218)). Their lookup budgets differ by algorithm; this is not an equal-compute training comparison.
   - **OK — mask construction and positions.** `bq == bk` plus causal offsets excludes other query blocks; positions repeat `range(P, P + block_len)`, matching single lookup. I read shared `_clone`: it clones key/value tensors ([scan_forward.py:472](/Users/neo/repos/polyformer-wt/scan/research/scan/scan_forward.py:472), [527](/Users/neo/repos/polyformer-wt/scan/research/scan/scan_forward.py:527), [577](/Users/neo/repos/polyformer-wt/scan/research/scan/scan_forward.py:577); [pb_pin.py:217](/Users/neo/repos/polyformer-wt/scan/research/promptbox/pb_pin.py:217)).
   - **DESIGN FLAW — certification scope.** The grid checks widths **1/8/32/full chunk**, repeated identical rows and one cyclic mixture. It does not bound every deployed mixture or partial width. Runtime margins are checked, but runtime Δlogits are not measured against single forwards ([scan_forward.py:700](/Users/neo/repos/polyformer-wt/scan/research/scan/scan_forward.py:700), [714](/Users/neo/repos/polyformer-wt/scan/research/scan/scan_forward.py:714), [932](/Users/neo/repos/polyformer-wt/scan/research/scan/scan_forward.py:932)). This supports empirical agreement, not a universal floating-point certificate.
   - **OK as forward timing; DESIGN FLAW as end-to-end latency.** `_sync()` brackets `model(...)`; mask creation, cache cloning and tensor setup occur before timing, and argmax extraction afterward. Prefills are also outside these totals. Counted runs execute certification first, so these are warmed measurements; there is no separate compile/warm-up accounting ([scan_forward.py:528](/Users/neo/repos/polyformer-wt/scan/research/scan/scan_forward.py:528), [539](/Users/neo/repos/polyformer-wt/scan/research/scan/scan_forward.py:539); [scan_job.py:182](/Users/neo/repos/polyformer-wt/scan/research/scan/scan_job.py:182)).
   - **OK — exhaustive fault set; DESIGN FLAW beyond that set.** The set is explicitly `"each of 9 rows replaced by each of the 2 wrong symbols"`. It tests single-entry symbol substitutions, not arbitrary corruption or repair; four associative but incorrect tables evade detection ([scan_algebra.py:90](/Users/neo/repos/polyformer-wt/scan/research/scan/scan_algebra.py:90); [SCAN.md:445](/Users/neo/repos/polyformer-wt/scan/research/scan/SCAN.md:445)).

5. **Figures — OK / not applicable.** These reports contain text and tables, not plotted figures; the report builder assembles those sections directly ([scan_report.py:1207](/Users/neo/repos/polyformer-wt/scan/research/scan/scan_report.py:1207)). The memory table has a numerical interpretation bug below.

**BUGs, by severity**

- **High — false corruption theorem.** `"A K↔P error does not change any sum"` is false ([SCAN.md:445](/Users/neo/repos/polyformer-wt/scan/research/scan/SCAN.md:445)). Independent replay gives **1009 + 1001 → 3010** under HS with `KK→P`, versus exact **2010**. All five “same-class” mutations have short HS counterexamples: `GKK`, `GKP`, `GPK`, `GPP`, `GGK`. Fix the theorem and test both parallel schedules: the number capable of damaging some HS sum becomes **18, not 13**; **14/18 detection remains unchanged**. The current test checks only sequential `S.add_with` ([test_scan_algebra.py:96](/Users/neo/repos/polyformer-wt/scan/research/scan/test_scan_algebra.py:96)).
- **Medium — bitwise certification can approve incorrect/tied outputs.** The branch sets `decision_ok = True` unconditionally ([scan_forward.py:753](/Users/neo/repos/polyformer-wt/scan/research/scan/scan_forward.py:753)). Executing that branch with equal logits, zero margin and one mismatch returns true. Require positive margins and zero target mismatches in both branches. **No change to the recorded margin-branch results.**
- **Medium — “margin holds” can mean “not checked.”** Million and L4 calls pass `None`; `margin_holds` defaults true ([scan_job.py:252](/Users/neo/repos/polyformer-wt/scan/research/scan/scan_job.py:252), [280](/Users/neo/repos/polyformer-wt/scan/research/scan/scan_job.py:280); [scan_forward.py:931](/Users/neo/repos/polyformer-wt/scan/research/scan/scan_forward.py:931)). Require a validated bound or report null. **Exact counts remain unchanged; these certification flags need qualification.**
- **Low — smoke extrapolation undercounts forwards.** It rounds total HS work once instead of rounding each dependent round ([scan_job.py:115](/Users/neo/repos/polyformer-wt/scan/research/scan/scan_job.py:115)). Correct count: **917**, not **914**; extrapolation **147.288910 s**, and ×1.25 threshold **184.111138 s**. Actual runs pass either threshold.
- **Low — resident KV omits retained Π.** `"Resident KV"` counts only Π+chunk, although `_clone` retains the original Π too ([HANDOFF_PAGE.md:21](/Users/neo/repos/polyformer-wt/scan/research/scan/HANDOFF_PAGE.md:21); [pb_pin.py:228](/Users/neo/repos/polyformer-wt/scan/research/promptbox/pb_pin.py:228)). Minimum combined KV is **721,944,576 / 614,596,608 / 957,874,176 bytes** for D/M/S, before temporaries. Relabel as working-cache size or include both caches.

**Headline claims that survive**

- Exact addition survives for all saved operands, including one million digits.
- Packed isolation and restarted positions survive code inspection; universal numerical certification does not.
- **~115 s survives as HS forward time; ~2.2 s does not—4,096 digits take ~3.52 s.**
- **14/18 detection survives**, restricted to the enumerated single-entry corruptions.
- The “five harmless K↔P corruptions” claim does not survive.

**Minimal re-runs:** no addition rerun is needed to establish saved-output exactness. Add CPU counterexamples for all five mutations and both schedules; fix certificate gates; rerun H100/L4 certification with saved per-row deltas, deployed mixtures and partial widths. Retime representative additions with synchronized end-to-end boundaries and explicit warm-up. Memory and extrapolation corrections require only recalculation.

   **DESIGN FLAW — incomplete raw certificate evidence.** I recomputed **446,253 comparisons** from row counts and widths, and the predicate **1.5 > 2×0.5 = 1**. But the saved certificate contains aggregate `"max_abs_delta"` and `"min_margin"`, not individual logit vectors: those extrema cannot be independently recomputed from these records ([cert.json:26](/Users/neo/repos/polyformer-wt/scan/research/scan/results/scan-counted2-h100/cert.json:26)). Likewise, the maximum saved cosine is **0.0208386555 < 0.3**, but that checks recorded pairwise values, not embeddings ([cert.json:122](/Users/neo/repos/polyformer-wt/scan/research/scan/results/scan-counted2-h100/cert.json:122)).

2. **Frozen rules — partly OK; one latent BUG.**

   The frozen text is reproduced unchanged. It requires `"greater than twice that maximum absolute difference"` ([registered_predictions.md:3](/Users/neo/repos/polyformer-wt/scan/research/scan/results/registered_predictions.md:3)); code correctly uses `bound = 2.0 * report["max_abs_delta"]` ([scan_forward.py:750](/Users/neo/repos/polyformer-wt/scan/research/scan/scan_forward.py:750)). **Δ < margin alone is insufficient**: both winning and competing logits can move.

   Predictions 2, 3 and 5 match the saved results; prediction 6 matches saved cosines. Original prediction 4, `"under 10 seconds"`, **fails**; the amended **183.509-second** threshold passes. Original prediction 1 failed on the first triple; the report correctly calls the selected-triple result `"a retest"` ([registered_predictions.md:5](/Users/neo/repos/polyformer-wt/scan/research/scan/results/registered_predictions.md:5), [9](/Users/neo/repos/polyformer-wt/scan/research/scan/results/registered_predictions.md:9); [SCAN.md:43](/Users/neo/repos/polyformer-wt/scan/research/scan/SCAN.md:43), [128](/Users/neo/repos/polyformer-wt/scan/research/scan/SCAN.md:128)). The bitwise branch violates the row-correctness requirement; see bugs below.

3. **Leakage — DESIGN FLAW for a generalization interpretation.**

   Every query’s labeled table row is deliberately present in Π: `_pi` executes `pi.extend(row)`, while queries use `row[:block_len]`. Thus this demonstrates execution of supplied lookup tables, not unseen-label arithmetic ([scan_forward.py:225](/Users/neo/repos/polyformer-wt/scan/research/scan/scan_forward.py:225), [258](/Users/neo/repos/polyformer-wt/scan/research/scan/scan_forward.py:258)). The S table also appears in adapter training: `"adder"` selects `adder_table(tok)` ([arith_data.py:246](/Users/neo/repos/polyformer-wt/scan/research/arith/arith_data.py:246)).

   Host code additionally supplies S’s carry-out using `cin_bits[i] + x + y >= 10`; it is a hybrid algorithm ([scan_forward.py:918](/Users/neo/repos/polyformer-wt/scan/research/scan/scan_forward.py:918)). The complete bigint answer is computed **after** model outputs and compared with full-string equality, not fed into them ([scan_forward.py:928](/Users/neo/repos/polyformer-wt/scan/research/scan/scan_forward.py:928)). Symbol selection evaluates the `"test"` name pool, so that pool is selection data, not an untouched holdout ([scan_forward.py:136](/Users/neo/repos/polyformer-wt/scan/research/scan/scan_forward.py:136), [403](/Users/neo/repos/polyformer-wt/scan/research/scan/scan_forward.py:403)).

4. **Comparison fairness, isolation and timing.**

   - **OK — common model/data.** Both schedules consume the same `d_ids` and tables ([scan_forward.py:893](/Users/neo/repos/polyformer-wt/scan/research/scan/scan_forward.py:893)). They share `adapter_step500`, rank **64**, training LR **0.0001**, **500 steps**; pinned, Mealy and CE/KL losses run from the training loop’s beginning ([scan_forward.py:19](/Users/neo/repos/polyformer-wt/scan/research/scan/scan_forward.py:19); [adapter_config.json:31](/Users/neo/repos/polyformer-wt/scan/research/arith/results/arith-s1r2/adapter_step500/adapter_config.json:31); [train_record.json:12](/Users/neo/repos/polyformer-wt/scan/research/arith/results/arith-s1r2/train_record.json:12), [30](/Users/neo/repos/polyformer-wt/scan/research/arith/results/arith-s1r2/train_record.json:30); [arith_train.py:197](/Users/neo/repos/polyformer-wt/scan/research/arith/arith_train.py:197), [218](/Users/neo/repos/polyformer-wt/scan/research/arith/arith_train.py:218)). Their lookup budgets differ by algorithm; this is not an equal-compute training comparison.
   - **OK — mask construction and positions.** `bq == bk` plus causal offsets excludes other query blocks; positions repeat `range(P, P + block_len)`, matching single lookup. I read shared `_clone`: it clones key/value tensors ([scan_forward.py:472](/Users/neo/repos/polyformer-wt/scan/research/scan/scan_forward.py:472), [527](/Users/neo/repos/polyformer-wt/scan/research/scan/scan_forward.py:527), [577](/Users/neo/repos/polyformer-wt/scan/research/scan/scan_forward.py:577); [pb_pin.py:217](/Users/neo/repos/polyformer-wt/scan/research/promptbox/pb_pin.py:217)).
   - **DESIGN FLAW — certification scope.** The grid checks widths **1/8/32/full chunk**, repeated identical rows and one cyclic mixture. It does not bound every deployed mixture or partial width. Runtime margins are checked, but runtime Δlogits are not measured against single forwards ([scan_forward.py:700](/Users/neo/repos/polyformer-wt/scan/research/scan/scan_forward.py:700), [714](/Users/neo/repos/polyformer-wt/scan/research/scan/scan_forward.py:714), [932](/Users/neo/repos/polyformer-wt/scan/research/scan/scan_forward.py:932)). This supports empirical agreement, not a universal floating-point certificate.
   - **OK as forward timing; DESIGN FLAW as end-to-end latency.** `_sync()` brackets `model(...)`; mask creation, cache cloning and tensor setup occur before timing, and argmax extraction afterward. Prefills are also outside these totals. Counted runs execute certification first, so these are warmed measurements; there is no separate compile/warm-up accounting ([scan_forward.py:528](/Users/neo/repos/polyformer-wt/scan/research/scan/scan_forward.py:528), [539](/Users/neo/repos/polyformer-wt/scan/research/scan/scan_forward.py:539); [scan_job.py:182](/Users/neo/repos/polyformer-wt/scan/research/scan/scan_job.py:182)).
   - **OK — exhaustive fault set; DESIGN FLAW beyond that set.** The set is explicitly `"each of 9 rows replaced by each of the 2 wrong symbols"`. It tests single-entry symbol substitutions, not arbitrary corruption or repair; four associative but incorrect tables evade detection ([scan_algebra.py:90](/Users/neo/repos/polyformer-wt/scan/research/scan/scan_algebra.py:90); [SCAN.md:445](/Users/neo/repos/polyformer-wt/scan/research/scan/SCAN.md:445)).

5. **Figures — OK / not applicable.** These reports contain text and tables, not plotted figures; the report builder assembles those sections directly ([scan_report.py:1207](/Users/neo/repos/polyformer-wt/scan/research/scan/scan_report.py:1207)). The memory table has a numerical interpretation bug below.

**BUGs, by severity**

- **High — false corruption theorem.** `"A K↔P error does not change any sum"` is false ([SCAN.md:445](/Users/neo/repos/polyformer-wt/scan/research/scan/SCAN.md:445)). Independent replay gives **1009 + 1001 → 3010** under HS with `KK→P`, versus exact **2010**. All five “same-class” mutations have short HS counterexamples: `GKK`, `GKP`, `GPK`, `GPP`, `GGK`. Fix the theorem and test both parallel schedules: the number capable of damaging some HS sum becomes **18, not 13**; **14/18 detection remains unchanged**. The current test checks only sequential `S.add_with` ([test_scan_algebra.py:96](/Users/neo/repos/polyformer-wt/scan/research/scan/test_scan_algebra.py:96)).
- **Medium — bitwise certification can approve incorrect/tied outputs.** The branch sets `decision_ok = True` unconditionally ([scan_forward.py:753](/Users/neo/repos/polyformer-wt/scan/research/scan/scan_forward.py:753)). Executing that branch with equal logits, zero margin and one mismatch returns true. Require positive margins and zero target mismatches in both branches. **No change to the recorded margin-branch results.**
- **Medium — “margin holds” can mean “not checked.”** Million and L4 calls pass `None`; `margin_holds` defaults true ([scan_job.py:252](/Users/neo/repos/polyformer-wt/scan/research/scan/scan_job.py:252), [280](/Users/neo/repos/polyformer-wt/scan/research/scan/scan_job.py:280); [scan_forward.py:931](/Users/neo/repos/polyformer-wt/scan/research/scan/scan_forward.py:931)). Require a validated bound or report null. **Exact counts remain unchanged; these certification flags need qualification.**
- **Low — smoke extrapolation undercounts forwards.** It rounds total HS work once instead of rounding each dependent round ([scan_job.py:115](/Users/neo/repos/polyformer-wt/scan/research/scan/scan_job.py:115)). Correct count: **917**, not **914**; extrapolation **147.288910 s**, and ×1.25 threshold **184.111138 s**. Actual runs pass either threshold.
- **Low — resident KV omits retained Π.** `"Resident KV"` counts only Π+chunk, although `_clone` retains the original Π too ([HANDOFF_PAGE.md:21](/Users/neo/repos/polyformer-wt/scan/research/scan/HANDOFF_PAGE.md:21); [pb_pin.py:228](/Users/neo/repos/polyformer-wt/scan/research/promptbox/pb_pin.py:228)). Minimum combined KV is **721,944,576 / 614,596,608 / 957,874,176 bytes** for D/M/S, before temporaries. Relabel as working-cache size or include both caches.

**Headline claims that survive**

- Exact addition survives for all saved operands, including one million digits.
- Packed isolation and restarted positions survive code inspection; universal numerical certification does not.
- **~115 s survives as HS forward time; ~2.2 s does not—4,096 digits take ~3.52 s.**
- **14/18 detection survives**, restricted to the enumerated single-entry corruptions.
- The “five harmless K↔P corruptions” claim does not survive.

**Minimal re-runs:** no addition rerun is needed to establish saved-output exactness. Add CPU counterexamples for all five mutations and both schedules; fix certificate gates; rerun H100/L4 certification with saved per-row deltas, deployed mixtures and partial widths. Retime representative additions with synchronized end-to-end boundaries and explicit warm-up. Memory and extrapolation corrections require only recalculation.
